### [1] [How light interacts with matter, revisited](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10171)
*Giancarlo Reali*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 以经典电荷振子辐射的推迟波叠加为单一图像，配合场的时间演化动画与数值模拟，系统定量地解释从天空蓝色、折射、金属光泽到切伦科夫辐射、二次谐波产生等一系列光学现象，并在经典图景止步处讨论与量子理论的衔接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1968年Weisskopf在《Scientific American》上以少量公式和大量文字讲述光与物质相互作用；本文希望借助此后发展起来的计算与可视化工具重述同一故事，把"加总振子辐射的推迟波"这一核心物理图像变成可计算、可观察的定量画面，服务于本科教学与对日常光学现象感兴趣的读者。

Method: 将光设为经典电磁场、物质设为带电振子集合，所有现象均由各振子辐射的推迟波按其延迟与相位叠加得到；用数值方法计算并绘制场分布及其时间演化并做成动画；采用"薄片法"（sheet method）使费曼箭头定量化；用耦合偶极子模拟展示入射波在介质内部被抵消与替换的过程；推导置于独立专栏，并公开绘图与模拟所用的Python代码。

Result: 由同一图像统一给出：天空的蓝色与云的白色、透明度与折射率、Ewald-Oseen消光定理、反射与薄膜颜色、金属反射及其与半导体的颜色成因、偏振片、布儒斯特角与双折射、镜片与显微镜及眼睛的成像、少周期脉冲在平板中的传播、切伦科夫辐射、放大与二次谐波产生；耦合偶极子模拟直观呈现介质内部入射波被抵消并被替换。

Conclusion: 经典振子加推迟波叠加的框架足以统一解释绝大多数日常光学现象，并在经典图像失效之处（光子、自发辐射、极光禁戒谱线、视觉与光合作用、路径求和）自然过渡到量子理论；本文定位为面向本科生、教师及好奇读者的教学性综述，附推导专栏、动画与Python代码。

Abstract: In 1968 Victor Weisskopf explained in Scientific American how light interacts with matter, with a few formulas and many words. We tell the same story again with the tools that have since become available: the fields are computed and drawn, and their evolution in time is shown in animations. Light is described as a classical electromagnetic field and matter as a collection of charged oscillators; every phenomenon follows from adding up the retarded waves radiated by the oscillators, each with its proper delay and phase. From this single picture we obtain the blue of the sky and the white of the clouds, transparency and the refractive index, the Ewald-Oseen extinction theorem, reflection and the colours of thin films, why metals reflect and what gives metals and semiconductors their colours, polarizers, Brewster's angle and birefringence, the working of mirrors, lenses, microscopes and the eye, the propagation of a few-cycle pulse through a slab, Cherenkov radiation, amplification and second-harmonic generation. Feynman's arrows are made quantitative with the sheet method, and coupled-dipole simulations show the incident wave being cancelled and replaced inside the medium. The connection with quantum theory is discussed where the classical picture stops: the photon, spontaneous emission and the forbidden lines of the aurora, vision and photosynthesis, and the sum over paths. The text is addressed to undergraduate students and their teachers, and to any reader curious about the physics behind everyday optical phenomena; derivations are collected in separate panels, and the animations and the Python codes used for the figures and simulations are provided as ancillary files.

</details>


### [2] [Adaptive wavefront shaping through flexible multicore photonic lantern fibres](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10290)
*Robert J. Kilpatrick,Alasdair Milne,Kerrianne Harrington,Thomas A. Wright,José C. A. Rocha,Aurélien Benoît,Helen Parker,Chaitanya K. Mididoddi,James M. Stone,Tim A. Birks,Robert R. Thomson,David B. Phillips*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出"光子灯笼光纤"架构，在保偏多芯光纤末端接锥形光子灯笼与短段多模光纤，实现高保真结构光投射，并在光纤任意弯折后无需接近远端即可重建高对比度聚焦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全息显微内窥镜可全光地将图像经发丝粗细光纤传输，但现有方案难以应对动态柔性弯折场景；传统多芯光纤因纤芯空间分离带来混叠（aliasing），限制了对远端光场的控制精度。

Method: 构建"光子灯笼光纤"：主体为低模态串扰的保偏多芯光纤，末端经锥形区（光子灯笼）耦合至短段多模光纤；利用波前整形向多模远端面投射高保真结构光；并提出两种原位弯曲补偿策略——远端安装的引导星（guide-star）与远端面反射光分析，实现无需接触远端的高对比度聚焦重建。

Result: 实现了优于传统多芯光纤的光学控制能力（突破纤芯分离导致的混叠限制），并在多芯光纤被任意扭曲后，仍可在输出面任意位置重建高对比度远端聚焦，且无需访问远端。

Conclusion: 该架构为经动态柔性光纤受控传输高维结构光场开辟新路径，并具备未来实现快速单帧（single-shot）弯曲标定的潜力，可推动移动生物体内成像、LiDAR与工业检测等应用。

Abstract: Holographic micro-endoscopy enables images to be funnelled all-optically through hair-thin strands of optical fibre. Future applications of this technology encompass biomedical imaging within mobile organisms, LiDAR, and industrial inspection. Yet holographic imaging through dynamically flexible optical fibres, called for in many real-world scenarios, represents an ongoing challenge. In this work we demonstrate how a bespoke fibre architecture that we term a 'photonic lantern fibre' offers a way forward. This device benefits from the negligible modal cross-talk of a polarisation-maintaining multicore fibre throughout the majority of its length. A tapered region at one end - known as a photonic lantern - guides light to a short segment of multimode optical fibre. We show how this fibre enables the projection of high-fidelity structured light to the multimode distal fibre facet via wavefront shaping, providing a level of optical control beyond that possible using conventional multicore fibres which suffer from aliasing due to their spatially separated cores. Furthermore, we demonstrate how high-contrast distal focussing can be re-established, anywhere across the output facet, after the multicore fibre is arbitrarily contorted, without access to the distal end. To achieve this we introduce two in-situ bend compensation strategies, that rely on a distally mounted guide-star or analysis of light reflected from the distal facet - offering the future potential for rapid single-shot bend calibration. Our work paves a new route towards the controlled delivery of high-dimensional structured light fields through dynamically flexible optical fibres.

</details>


### [3] [Intensity limits for phase-only holographic optical-tweezer arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10302)
*Anzhou Wen,Hengyu Lian,Kevin Yipu Wu,Maxwell F. Parsons,Praneeth Chakravarthula*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该文推导了纯相位全息图在光镊阵列中分配光能量的效率上界，揭示了照明方式、陷阱几何与SLM像素衍射对最小强度极限的影响，并给出现有优化已接近理论极限的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将光镊扩展到大规模阵列需要为每个陷阱提供足够光强，同时最小化总激光功率；然而纯相位全息图分配光的效率极限尚不明确，难以判断进一步提升相位优化是否还有收益。

Method: 从理论上推导陷阱位点最小强度的上界，综合考虑照明条件、目标几何构型以及空间光调制器（SLM）像素衍射；针对单平面阵列与跨两个轴向平面的阵列，分析极限随陷阱数与SLM像素数的缩放规律，并建立三维聚焦条件；通过数值实验验证优化全息图与理论极限的接近程度。

Result: 优化后的全息图能紧密逼近理论极限，说明仅靠相位优化带来的功率节省已十分有限；同时表明照明方式与陷阱几何的选择对光效率有显著影响。

Conclusion: 相位优化本身已接近饱和，提升光镊阵列功率效率的关键在于选择合适的照明与陷阱几何构型，为设计更大规模、更低功耗的光镊阵列提供了实用准则。

Abstract: Optical tweezers use tightly focused laser beams to trap and manipulate individual particles, enabling advances in quantum computing, drug discovery, and biological research. Scaling these technologies to large arrays requires delivering sufficient light to every trapping site while minimizing the total laser power required. Here, we investigate the fundamental limits of how efficiently a phase-only hologram can distribute light across an array of optical traps. We derive upper bounds on the minimum intensity across trapping sites, accounting for illumination, target geometry, and diffraction from spatial light modulator pixels. For arrays arranged in a single plane or across two axial planes, we show how these limits scale with the number of traps and SLM pixels, and establish conditions for focusing light in three dimensions. Numerical experiments demonstrate that optimized holograms can closely approach these theoretical limits, suggesting that further improvements in phase optimization alone offer limited power savings. Instead, our results reveal how the choice of illumination and trap geometry can improve light efficiency, providing practical guidelines for designing larger, more power-efficient optical-tweezer arrays.

</details>


### [4] [Dynamic Molecular Transformation Mediated Crystallization Regulation and Interface Passivation in Tin Based Perovskite Solar Cells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10420)
*Ying Tang,Zuhong Zhang,Hongzhuo Wu,Zhihao Li,Zhenhuang Su,Yonggang Yang,Hairui Liu,Feng Yang,Zhe Li,Luca Sapienza,Jorge Pascual,Shengnan Zuo,Bo Hou,Antonio Abate,Meng Li,Yufang Liu*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该文用1-乙酰哌嗪(PX)实现"动态分子转变"：成核阶段羰基配位Sn²⁺调控结晶，退火时PX原位转化为脒基功能物种占据表面A位空位实现钝化，兼顾结晶控制与界面钝化，锡基钙钛矿器件效率达15.18%，500 h连续光照后保持94.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 锡基钙钛矿是无铅光伏的有前景候选，但存在Sn²⁺配位不稳定、结晶过快以及严重界面缺陷等问题，且结晶调控与界面钝化通常需分步、分添加剂处理，缺乏一体化策略。

Method: 引入1-乙酰哌嗪(PX)作为单一功能分子：成核与生长阶段，其羰基与Sn²⁺配位以稳定前驱体络合物、降低过饱和度并实现可控晶体生长；退火过程中PX发生原位分子转变，推测生成脒基功能化物种，优先占据表面A位空位形成钝化层。

Result: 获得致密、高结晶度的钙钛矿薄膜并改善光电质量；器件PCE达15.18%，连续光照500 h后仍保持初始效率的94.1%，非辐射复合被有效抑制。

Conclusion: 提出"体相—界面"耦合的动态分子工程策略，将结晶调控与界面钝化整合于同一分子体系，为高效稳定的无铅钙钛矿光伏提供可行路径。

Abstract: Tin-based perovskites are promising candidates for lead-free photovoltaics but suffer from labile Sn$^{2+}$ coordination, rapid crystallization, and severe interfacial defects. Here, we develop a dynamic molecular transformation using 1-acetylpiperazine (PX) to regulate crystallization and interface chemistry simultaneously. During nucleation and growth, the carbonyl group coordinates with Sn$^{2+}$ to stabilize precursor complexes, moderate supersaturation, and enable controlled crystal growth. Upon annealing, PX undergoes in situ molecular transformation, suggested to form amidinium-functionalized species that preferentially occupy surface A-site vacancies, forming a passivation layer and suppressing nonradiative recombination. This coupled bulk-to-interface evolution yields compact, highly crystalline films with improved optoelectronic quality. Devices achieve a power conversion efficiency of 15.18% and retain 94.1% of initial efficiency after 500 h of continuous illumination. This work demonstrates a dynamic molecular engineering approach integrating crystallization control and interface passivation, providing a pathway toward efficient and stable lead-free perovskite photovoltaics.

</details>


### [5] [Wideband AR-HCF Transmission Under Variable Network Traffic: A System-Level Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10470)
*Md Ghulam Saber,Zhiping Jiang*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该文用逐跨模型比较AR-HCF与SMF在动态业务下频谱占用变化对残留信道的影响，发现AR-HCF把弹性宽带传输的约束从光纤非线性转移到放大器站点器件、气体吸收和控制动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态业务使宽带光链路频谱占用变化，通过NLI、受激拉曼散射和放大器负载扰动SMF中的残留信道，因此需要ASE填充维持满占用。作者要检验在AR-HCF中频谱填充是否仍必要，并厘清受限因素。

Method: 构建96信道、9.6-THz扩展C+L、960 km链路的逐跨仿真模型，联合追踪信号、ASE与NLI，并纳入放大器尾纤、逐线CO2吸收、模间干扰、放大器控制与负载瞬态；对比100%与20%占用下残留信道GSNR变化。

Result: 等发射功率下占用由100%降至20%，AR-HCF的GSNR仅变化0.0016 dB，SMF为1.52 dB；在AR-HCF吞吐最优的+12.3 dBm/信道处升至0.24 dB，主要来自短固体玻璃放大器尾纤（尤其输出尾纤）的NLI。去除尾纤仅增约1%吞吐。逐线气体建模分辨出数dB CO2衰减（多被逐跨均衡抵消）与平坦增益无法补偿的窄吸收陷波。恒增益控制可节能23%，恒输出控制无节能；85%信道掉线造成8.8-dB无控增益偏移，500-Hz环路降至0.6 dB。

Conclusion: AR-HCF中频谱填充的必要性显著降低，弹性宽带传输的主要约束由光纤非线性转向放大器站点器件、气体吸收与控制动态。

Abstract: Dynamic traffic changes spectral occupancy in wideband optical links, perturbing surviving channels in single-mode fiber (SMF) through nonlinear interference (NLI), inter-channel stimulated Raman scattering, and amplifier loading. This motivates amplified-spontaneous-emission (ASE) idlers to maintain full occupancy. We test whether spectral filling remains necessary in antiresonant hollow-core fiber (AR-HCF) using a span-by-span model of a 96-channel, 9.6-THz extended-C+L link over 960 km. The model jointly tracks signal, ASE, and NLI, including amplifier pigtails, line-resolved CO2 absorption, intermodal interference, amplifier control, and load transients. At equal launch power, reducing occupancy from 100% to 20% changes surviving-channel generalized signal-to-noise ratio (GSNR) by just 0.0016 dB in AR-HCF versus 1.52 dB in SMF. At the AR-HCF throughput-optimal launch of +12.3 dBm/channel, the GSNR shift rises to 0.24 dB, nearly all from NLI in short solid-glass amplifier pigtails, chiefly the output pigtail. These pigtails hold the optimum 8.9 dB below the amplifier ceiling, but removing them yields only about 1% more throughput at that launch due to transceiver, intermodal-interference, and gas-absorption limits. Line-resolved gas modeling distinguishes several decibels of CO2 attenuation, largely offset by per-span equalization, from narrow absorption notches not recoverable by flat gain. Constant-gain control permits a 23% amplifier energy saving by tracking load, whereas constant-output control saves none. An 85% channel drop causes an 8.8-dB uncontrolled gain excursion, reduced to 0.6 dB with an assumed 500-Hz loop. Thus, AR-HCF shifts the constraints on elastic wideband transmission from fiber nonlinearities to amplifier-site components, gas absorption, and control dynamics.

</details>


### [6] [Dynamics of squeezed-state preparation in quadratic and Kerr nonlinear microresonators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10504)
*S. D. Chikalkin,V. L. Gorshenin,V. E. Lobanov,I. A. Bilenko,D. A. Chermoshentsev*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 建立含泵浦反作用的开放量子系统动力学模型，描述非线性微腔中压缩态的产生，给出未耗尽泵浦近似的有效范围并预测可突破3 dB极限的瞬态上阈值压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准未耗尽泵浦近似忽略了信号模对泵浦模的反作用，无法确定该近似的有效范围，也限制了阈值以上压缩动力学的分析。

Method: 将谐振腔视为开放量子系统，泵浦模用含时经典振幅描述以纳入泵浦耗尽；对χ⁽²⁾微腔建立动力学模型，并推广至含两个泵浦模的三模Kerr微腔；另用固定泵浦振幅的约化五模模型研究稳态压缩，其中包含寄生单泵浦自发四波混频通道。

Result: χ⁽²⁾模型在阈值以下复现常规3 dB腔内压缩极限，并预测在泵浦耗尽显著之前有限时间内可超越该极限的瞬态上阈值区间；寄生通道降低可达压缩度，并将不稳定阈值移至更低泵浦振幅。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个紧凑框架，用于估计未耗尽泵浦近似的有效范围并分析非线性微腔中的瞬态压缩动力学。

Abstract: We develop a dynamical model of squeezed-state generation in nonlinear optical microresonators that takes into account the back-action of the signal mode on the pump mode. The resonator is treated as an open quantum system, while the pump modes are described by time-dependent classical amplitudes, which allows pump depletion to be included beyond the standard undepleted-pump approximation. For a $χ^{(2)}$ microresonator, the model reproduces the conventional 3~dB intracavity squeezing limit below threshold and predicts a transient above-threshold regime in which this limit can be overcome for a finite time before pump depletion becomes appreciable. The dynamical approach is extended to a three-mode Kerr microresonator with two pump modes. Separately, we investigate stationary squeezing in a reduced five-mode model with fixed pump amplitudes, including a parasitic single-pump spontaneous four-wave-mixing channel. Within this model, the parasitic channel reduces the achievable squeezing and shifts the instability threshold to lower pump amplitudes. These results provide a compact framework for estimating the validity range of the undepleted-pump approximation and for analyzing transient squeezing dynamics in nonlinear microresonators.

</details>


### [7] [Coupled plasmonic Fabry-Perot resonances modulating electromagnetic enhancement at a silver nanowire-nanoparticle junction proved by surface-enhanced fluorescence spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10534)
*Tamitake Itoh,Yuko S. Yamamoto*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 研究了银纳米颗粒-纳米线结区表面增强荧光(SEF)的电磁增强因子FR谱，发现其呈现由NW内两个等离激元Fabry-Pérot谐振耦合导致的振荡调制，并建立耦合FP谐振解析模型予以解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SEF是染料分子表面增强共振拉曼散射下的宽背景谱，其电磁增强因子的光谱特性此前缺乏系统刻画与物理机制解释，尤其是结区几何（NP位置、NW长度）如何调控增强谱尚不明确。

Method: 测量Ag NPs–NWs结区发射的SEF并提取FR谱，考察发射偏振（垂直/平行NW长轴）、NW长度（长/短）与NP位置（中心/端部）的依赖；结合位置依赖FR谱的数值计算；进而构建以实验测得NW长度与NP位置为输入、可变谐振腔间耦合效率的耦合FP谐振解析模型。

Result: FR谱呈复杂振荡结构，偏振垂直于NW长轴时更显著；长NW给出精细振荡、短NW给出宽振荡，NP靠近端部时二者叠加；数值计算成功复现这些位置依赖特征；解析模型揭示复杂谱源于两个FP谐振的干涉与叠加，其能量由NP局域等离激元辐射。

Conclusion: SEF谱的光谱调制可作为表征NW内FP腔模耦合的探针；NP分隔的两个等离激元FP谐振腔的干涉与叠加是复杂FR谱的起源，耦合FP模型能定量描述该效应。

Abstract: We investigated the surface-enhanced fluorescence (SEF), which is the broad background spectrum underlying the surface-enhanced resonant Raman scattering of dye molecules, emitted from junctions between silver nanoparticles (NPs) and nanowires (NWs). We found that the electromagnetic enhancement factors (FR) derived from the SEF exhibited spectral modulation, such as complicated oscillatory structures. This property became clearer when the emission polarization was perpendicular to the long axis of the NWs. The FR spectra of the long and short NWs exhibit fine and broad oscillatory structures, respectively, when the NPs are positioned near the NW centers. When the NPs were near the NW ends, a combination of fine and broad structures was observed. Numerical calculations of the position-dependent FR spectra successfully reproduced these properties, indicating that the spectral properties originated from the interaction between two plasmonic Fabry-Perot (FP) resonators within the NW, separated by a NP. Based on this result, an analytical model of coupled FP resonators was developed to evaluate these spectral features using experimentally measured NW lengths and NP positions while varying the inter-resonator coupling efficiency. This analysis revealed that the interference and superposition of the two FP resonances resulted in these complex spectra, the energy of which was radiated by the localized plasmons of the NPs.

</details>


### [8] [Precision-informed Fourier compression of refractive index tomograms with voxel-wise error control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10663)
*Dohyeon Lee,Juheon Lee,Sehyeon Lee,Juyeon Park,YongKeun Park*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出一种基于傅里叶空间量化的体数据编码方案，对全息断层（HT）重建的三维折射率（RI）体积实现高压缩率，并在指定误差预算下进行逐体素验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Holotomography 产生的三维折射率体数据与时间序列体量庞大，需要紧凑存储，同时必须对重建数据的数值保真度进行可控、可验证的约束。

Method: 直接在傅里叶空间编码重建的三维RI体：用量化傅里叶系数表征体结构，配合空间残差量化，并采用1%安全裕度而不逐体素存储独立校正；通过逐体素验证对照给定误差预算。评估涵盖细胞与类器官数据、并发处理，以及向介电张量层析与吸收层析的扩展。

Abstract: Significance: Holotomography (HT) generates large refractive index (RI) volumes and time series, creating a need for compact storage with controlled numerical fidelity. Approach: Quantized Fourier coefficients represent volumetric structure, while spatial residual quantization uses a 1% safety margin without storing separate corrections for individual voxels. We evaluated cellular and organoid data, concurrent processing, and extensions to dielectric-tensor and absorption tomography. Results: At a budget of 0.0001 RI units, complete packets achieved compression ratios of 7.89-9.50 relative to float32 arrays and were 28-30% smaller than BitRound-Pcodec packets satisfying the same error limit. Concurrent processing reduced encoding and decoding wall times by factors of 5.14 and 4.81 without changing packets. The fixed margin passed all primary-budget tests but failed at one smaller budget in both precision-sweep inputs. Conclusions: Precision-informed volumetric Fourier coding reduces storage of holotomograms with verified voxel-wise fidelity at the primary budget, at additional computational cost.

Abstract: MXenes - a rapidly expanding family of two-dimensional transition-metal carbides, nitrides, and carbonitrides - have emerged as powerful nonlinear photonic materials owing to their metallic conductivity, ultrafast carrier dynamics, broadband optical response, and highly tunable surface chemistry. This Review provides the first comprehensive overview of the nonlinear optical (NLO) properties of MXenes and their growing impact on advanced photonic technologies. We discuss state-of-the-art synthesis strategies, from fluoride-based etching to molten-salt, electrochemical, and CVD-enabled routes, highlighting how structural quality and surface terminations govern optical behaviour. A detailed analysis of third-order nonlinearities including Kerr effects, two- and multi-photon absorption, saturable and reverse-saturable absorption, and harmonic generation is presented, supported by quantitative comparisons across MXene families. We further examine modelling approaches linking band structure to nonlinear response and survey application frontiers such as gas sensing, ultrafast lasers, all-optical limiters, and emerging photonic neural networks. Finally, we outline future research directions spanning material engineering, device integration, and quantum/nonlinear metasurface platforms. Together, these insights position MXenes as a transformative materials platform for next-generation nonlinear photonics and ultrafast all-optical information processing.

Abstract: Two-dimensional (2D) materials have emerged as an extraordinary platform for polaritonics, given their remarkable ability to confine light to deep sub-wavelength scales. Starting from the quantum properties of a polarizable 2D surface, we derive collective excitations and we identify both transverse electric magnetic modes existing outside the light cone. Our results demonstrate that these 2D systems facilitate a powerful synergy between extreme wavelength shrinking and intrinsic quantum squeezing of electric field quadratures. Abstract: Chirality in plasmonic systems is commonly associated with structures differentiated by their handedness. We attribute this effect to the transfer of the optical angular momentum in the system. While an individual sub-wavelength circular aperture is not expected to alter the total angular momentum, the periodic structure results in a coherent coupling of plasmons wavefronts, giving rise to a collective Bloch-Floquet-like response. The edge of the structure accumulates the helical phase that eventually results in a visible spin-dependent shifting of the plasmonic beam. We systematically study the effects of hole-array size, periodicity and the geometry. The observed phenomenon shows an unprecedented robustness and consistency. These results establish periodic plasmonic arrays as a platform for converting localized helicity-dependent responses into collective chirality, providing a route toward controlling chiral plasmonic wavefronts in structurally achiral systems.

Method: 以评论（Comment）形式对原文方案进行理论辨析，对比原发明与所提变体在温差能力上的差异，并分析温差对相位差补偿的作用。

Abstract: This comment addresses the publication [Appl. Opt. 65, 7090 (2026)] which targets an alleged ``optimization'' of a patented invention conceived by the author. The invention had introduced a multi-temperature management scheme for a dual-wavelength-resonant nonlinear cavity that optimized the crystal temperature around the beam waist for phase-matching, while utilizing independently adjustable temperatures in other regions of the same crystal to simultaneously satisfy the double-resonance condition. A foundational form of this invention was initially utilized by co-author H. Vahlbruch without the author's prior knowledge during the construction of the squeezed-light laser for the Virgo gravitational wave detector. In their publication, the three authors now propose a variant that reduces the maximum achievable temperature differential. Here, I demonstrate that this approach does not constitute an optimization but, on the contrary, represents a regression that dismantles the invention. In general, a high-quality implementation of this invention fundamentally depends on maximizing the achievable temperature differential. Only then can the problematic phase differences---introduced by dielectric coatings, Gouy phase shifts, and suboptimal poling lengths at the crystal facets---be effectively compensated for, particularly in short crystals. If desired, smaller temperature differences can always be selected dynamically anyway.

</details>

Abstract: The polarization state of a supercontinuum (SC) vector beam provides a reference for quantitative polarimetry. Representative maps show an approximately tangential azimuthal angle and a small ellipticity parameter over much of the beam, together with the degree of polarization. The {\pm} 90° azimuthal angle boundary shifts by about 21° between 450 and 766 nm. A linear fit gives 21° {\pm} 7°, where the uncertainty is derived from the slope confidence interval and does not include uncharacterized systematic errors. Jones-matrix calculations examine candidate imperfections under specified assumptions. Attributing the measured boundary shift to this mechanism requires an intensity extinction ratio of about 7 or less at 766 nm if the offset at 450 nm is negligible and the shift corresponds to a uniform azimuthal angle change. The origin remains unresolved. The results motivate wavelength-dependent characterization and calibration of this probe for quantitative polarimetry.

Abstract: Recent advances have shown that thermal radiation can exhibit strong polarization in both the near and far fields. However, most existing studies focus on paraxial far-field emission, where polarization can be described using conventional two-dimensional (2D) Stokes parameters. Non-paraxial thermal radiation, with three nonzero vector components, requires a fully three-dimensional (3D) polarization description. This work develops a generalized theoretical method for analyzing thermal-radiation polarization at arbitrary distances from an emitter. Despite recent observations of intricate self-synchronization structures in mode-locked lasers-such as subharmonic entrainment, devil's staircases, and holes within Arnold tongues, i.e., regions of unsynchronized dynamics embedded within synchronized domains-their physical origin has remained elusive due to the complexity of laser dynamics. Abstract: Near-perfect light absorption in ultrathin films is hindered by two fundamental obstacles: the momentum mismatch that prevents resonant excitation of surface exciton-polaritons (SEPs) under ordinary illumination, and the 50% absorption ceiling imposed on free-standing conductive sheets by geometric symmetry. Here we show that both obstacles can be overcome simultaneously in a simple, planar geometry. A one-dimensional periodic grating of anisotropic polarizable ribbons composed of J-aggregates supporting bright molecular excitons provides the reciprocal lattice momentum needed to excite SEPs without employing an attenuated total reflection configuration, while a metallic mirror beneath a dielectric spacer folds the reflected field back onto the absorber, breaking the $50\%$ limit through interference between the incident and reflected waves. We develop a semi-analytical matrix formulation to compute the full optical response of such structure, and demonstrate that the absorptance at the first-order SEP resonance can be continuously tuned from near-unity to near-zero by varying the spacer thickness alone. The grating period and filling factor offer additional spectral control, and oblique incidence unlocks a second, out-of-plane polariton branch. These results establish periodic gratings of excitonic media as a versatile, practical and integrable platform for spectrally selective, near-perfect light absorption.

Abstract: We report minute-timescale time-resolved measurements of X-ray parametric down-conversion (PDC) of X-rays into ultraviolet photons in a MAPbBr3 single crystal under continuous irradiation. Despite the known radiation sensitivity of hybrid perovskites and the high photon flux required to detect the intrinsically weak PDC signal, the nonlinear response remains measurable throughout the irradiation sequence. Both PDC and Bragg diffraction exhibit a slow decay consistent with diffusion-driven damage dynamics on timescales reported for bromide-vacancy and proton migration. However, despite their similar decay rates, the PDC and Bragg signals evolve toward markedly different steady states, while an additional fast decay component is observed in the Bragg response but absent in the PDC signal. Density functional theory calculations identify N 2s states of the methylammonium cation as the most likely contributors to the measured PDC response and show that their Fourier component is only weakly affected by bromide-vacancy formation. TL;DR: 本文提出将探测器位置作为相干单像素成像(CSPI)的额外优化自由度：在傅里叶面上选择适当的离焦位置可在抑制涨落敏感的相干背景的同时保留空间编码信号，从而提升重建保真度；并用太赫兹液晶空间光调制器与相干外差探测实验验证，对称离焦对通过相干通道融合进一步提升成像质量。

Abstract: Coherent single-pixel imaging (CSPI) enables amplitude- and phase-resolved computational imaging with a single coherent detector, offering particular advantages in spectral regimes where high-performance detector arrays remain unavailable. In conventional Fourier-plane CSPI, the detector is typically placed at the focal point to maximize the collected power. However, this focal position does not necessarily maximize the reconstruction fidelity for image reconstruction in the presence of system nonidealities. Here, we introduce detector position as an additional degree of freedom for CSPI optimization. Experimentally, using a programmable liquid-crystal terahertz spatial light modulator and coherent heterodyne detection, we demonstrate improved amplitude and phase reconstruction from selected off-focus channels compared with focal detection. A symmetric off-focus pair with complementary transverse-wave-vector weighting further enhances image fidelity, contrast, and compressive-sampling robustness through coherent channel fusion. These results establish detector-position engineering as a new strategy for enhancing the robustness and information efficiency of CSPI.

Abstract: Optical frequency combs, consisting of mutually coherent and equally spaced spectral lines, provide a powerful means of synthesizing, distributing, and processing optical frequencies on chip. Among integrated photonic platforms, thin-film lithium niobate (TFLN) is particularly attractive for comb generation because it combines strong electro-optic (EO) functionality, accessible $χ^{(2)}$ and $χ^{(3)}$ nonlinearities, low-loss nanophotonic confinement, and versatile dispersion engineering. This review summarizes the physical foundations and representative realizations of TFLN combs, and discusses their applications in optical communication, microwave photonics, optical computing, and precision metrology. The integration of nonlinear functionality, EO control, and passive photonic components is expected to advance compact, energy-efficient comb sources and broaden the utility of TFLN in integrated photonics and quantum optics.

Abstract: Burst-mode laser processing has gained increasing attention in material machining due to its potential to enhance processing efficiency and quality.   <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Topological phases, exceptional points, and bound states in the continuum (BICs) are three key concepts in condensed matter and classical wave physics. Their pairwise combinations have yielded a rich variety of intriguing singular modes, but the triple merger--exceptional topological BICs (ET-BICs)--has yet to be explored. In this Letter, we theoretically propose and experimentally demonstrate a universal approach for constructing such elusive exceptional structures. The system consists of four coupled topological chains: y-mirror stacking generates pairs of topological BICs, while z-stacking with biased loss yields a generalized mirror symmetry under which each pair--separated in different subspaces until their collapse--merges into an ET-BIC at a critical loss. Experimentally, using an acoustic platform with electrically tunable loss, we not only directly observe the coalescence of eigenenergies but also the coalescence of eigenstates, offering a complete verification of the ET-BIC formation. The triply synergistic ET-BICs may pave the way for wave devices that simultaneously demand robustness, sensitivity, and BIC confinement.

Abstract: One-dimensional nanowires can host remarkable electronic properties and are central to studying quantum transport. Experiments on lithographically defined one-dimensional nanowires therefore do not exhibit ideal behavior. Using theoretical and computational models, we predict that optimizing the geometry of split gates in semiconducting heterostructures will lead to higher-quality channels for transport measurements. Further, we show that this optimization is essential for resolving features of interest such as the Rashba-induced spin gap. This new approach to lithographically defined conduction channels provides better energy resolution and is less susceptible to disorder.

Abstract: Probing the change in conductance or reactance of a charge sensitive system using an rf cavity is a standard way of doing fast single-charge electrometry. In this article, we present a detector concept which is instead based on coherent interaction. We show that the coherent interaction between a microwave resonator and double quantum dot gives rise to a fast, highly sensitive electrometer. The coherent electrometer achieves a state-of-the-art readout speed, resolving two charge states with SNR of 11.7 in 16 $μ$s, with a readout signal two orders of magnitude lower in power than previously required to reach this speed. In this way, heating of the sample becomes negligible. Thanks to its sensitivity and low dissipation, the coherent electrometer opens up the avenue for quantum limited charge detection with nanosecond speed and minimal back-action.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典两端口单通道no-go定理依赖时间反演对称性(TRS)与散射矩阵幺正性。开放体系中遗漏通道或环境泄漏破坏幺正性，使TRS与辛互易对称性TRS\(^{\dagger}\)不再等价，经典前提失效，因此需要为非幺正情形建立新的判据。

Method: 理论上推导：证明当两端口单通道散射矩阵同时满足TRS\(^{\dagger}\)与中心反演对称时，透射无自旋选择性，从而作为经典no-go定理在非幺正区间的补充；分析条件被破坏或解除单通道约束时自旋选择性输运的出现。数值/模型验证：以手性γ-环糊精分子组装体的电荷输运为对象，分别检验幺正情形的经典定理与非幺正情形的补充版本及其多通道推论。

Abstract: The classical two-terminal single-channel no-go theorem relies on time-reversal symmetry (TRS) and scattering-matrix unitarity. In open systems, omitted channels or environment leakage break unitarity, so TRS and the symplectic reciprocity symmetry TRS\(^{\dagger}\) are no longer equivalent, invalidating the classical premises. We prove that if the two-terminal single-channel scattering matrix satisfies TRS\(^{\dagger}\) plus central inversion symmetry, transmission remains no spin selectivity; this result provides an important complement to the classical no-go theorem in nonunitary regime. When these conditions are violated or the single-channel constraint is lifted, spin-selective transport occurs. In charge transport through devices based on chiral \(γ\)-cyclodextrin molecular assemblies, we verify separately the classical no-go theorem in unitary case and its supplementary version together with its multichannel corollary in nonunitary case: when the conditions for the corresponding no-go theorem hold, the chirality-induced spin selectivity (CISS) of the assembly disappears, whereas when they are violated, CISS emerges. These results extend the no-go theorem to nonunitary scattering systems and establish an effective theoretical framework for studying CISS in real molecular assemblies.

</details>

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the photonic spin Hall effect in a two-dimensional Rashba-coupled $d$-wave altermagnetic Chern system subjected to a uniform out-of-plane exchange field. We first analyze the energy-band structure and the Dirac mass distribution at the high-symmetry points, where $m_Γ=m_M=Δ_x, m_X=Δ_x-4 t_J$, and $m_Y=Δ_x+4 t_J$. Band inversions at high-symmetry points generate both a topologically trivial regime $(C=0)$ and Chern-insulating phases $(C= \pm 1)$. Employing the Kubo formalism for the optical conductivity together with the reflection matrix for a substrate-supported conducting sheet, we compute helicity-dependent finite-waist displacements of a reflected Gaussian beam. The resulting optical response exhibits a minimum interband absorption threshold, a Van Hove-type spectral feature, and distinct transitions at the $X$ and $Y$ points arising from the momentum dependence of the exchange field. Under the specified Hall-tensor and polarization sign conventions, the longitudinal helicity half-separation is odd upon reversal of the optical Hall conductivity and correspondingly changes sign between opposite-Chern phases, whereas the transverse half-separation is even in the Hall response. The maximal spin-dependent splitting occur in the vicinity of the pseudo-Brewster reflectance minimum. The longitudinal spin splitting reverses sign between opposite Chern phases, reflecting the exchange-controlled optical Hall response, while its pronounced spectral variations reveal the influence of inequivalent interband transitions induced by the $d$-wave altermagnetic exchange. These findings establish polarization-resolved photonic beam shifts as a sensitive probe of Hall-active topological phases and their characteristic optical spectra.

Abstract: Multidimensional coherent spectroscopy probes the response of a system to a sequence of time-separated light pulses, and has attracted significant interest in recent years. In the frequency space, it generates multidimensional spectra, in which information about different degrees of freedom of the system is generically disentangled. In this work, we develop a quantum geometric formulation of multidimensional optical susceptibilities in insulating systems. Focusing on a two-pulse setup and working in the velocity gauge, we derive analytical expressions for second-order and third-order two-dimensional coherent spectra (2DCS). We focus on the third-order ABB response, which remains finite in centrosymmetric systems where the second-order susceptibility vanishes by inversion symmetry. Its paramagnetic contribution supports an echo signal that probes products of four interband Berry-connection matrix elements, which reduce in two-band systems to bilinear combinations of the quantum geometric tensor of the occupied band. Using continuum and square-lattice models of two-dimensional Chern insulators, we show how the echo resolves the momentum-space sign structure of these geometric quantities. For the $xxyx$ response of the models considered, the number of nodes in the echo signal is related to the band topology. Our results extend the quantum-geometric interpretation of multidimensional spectroscopy to higher-order responses, showing that third-order 2DCS provides access to geometric structures beyond those probed at lower orders.

*J. C. G. Henriques,B. Viña-Bausá,I. Brihuega,J. Fernández-Rossier*

Finally, we reconcile the local character of IETS with its sensitivity to nonlocal entanglement through an analogy with digital quantum circuits, where Hamiltonian evolution implements multiqubit operations. These results establish IETS as a tool for quantifying and imaging entanglement in engineered on-surface spin systems.

Abstract: The photonic bath at infinity represents the unbounded electromagnetic environment as an effective reservoir in the nonequilibrium Green's function (NEGF) formalism. We develop four complementary approaches to deriving the bath self-energy: infinitesimal environmental absorption, semi-infinite leads, inversion of the free Green's function, and sesquilinear forms with outgoing radiation boundary conditions. In particular, we present an explicit three-site matrix inversion for a planar geometry and introduce a sesquilinear form that relates the bath self-energy to the Silver--Müller radiation condition. A precise analysis of their amplitude, which is modulated by the double resonance with PIROs, dramatically enhances the visibility of the latter compared to the equilibrium case. We identify transverse acoustic phonons with a sound velocity of $s = 3.4$\,km/s as the relevant mode and analyze the oscillatory amplitude modulation in the subsonic regime ($\varepsilon_{\mathrm{dc}} < \varepsilon_{\mathrm{ph}}$) within the theoretical framework of Dmitriev \textit{et al.} At the sound barrier ($\varepsilon_{\mathrm{dc}} = \varepsilon_{\mathrm{ph}}$), where the electron drift velocity reaches the speed of sound, we observe a phase shift in the oscillatory electron-phonon interaction that independently confirms recent experimental findings.

For low-energy plasmons, thermal excitation extends the accessible energy-momentum range while preserving high quality factors and collective coherence. Electrostatic doping yields distinct plasmon behaviors: moderate doping gives K-valley dominated intraband $\sqrt{q}$ Dirac plasmons; heavy electron doping retains $\sqrt{q}$ dispersion via Drude contributions from $Γ$ and K valleys; heavy hole doping triggers interband transitions near the $Γ$ point, producing new $σ$-$σ$ and $σ$-$σ^*$ interband plasmon modes and complex multi-branch spectra. Abstract: The bulk-boundary correspondence states that topological properties of a bulk material guarantee the existence of protected edge modes, oftentimes associated with the attractive property of backscattering immunity. In this Letter, we consider a time reversal invariant classical system with both translation and chiral, or sublattice, symmetries. We show that when these two symmetries anti-commute, the material exhibits a topological phase protected by the same invariant as type D topological superconductors. We realize such a system experimentally and observe the existence of the associated edge modes in an acoustic network. Our measurements are in excellent agreement with theoretical predictions from discrete lattice models. Finally, we show that these edge modes are immune to backscattering for perturbations consisting in two subsequent identical defects.

Abstract: Rhombohedral graphene multilayers provide a flexible platform for correlated physics associated with their flat-band electronic structure.While a variety of correlated states appear in these systems as a function of bias and electrostatic doping, promoting specific correlated flavor symmetry breaking states remains a challenge. Here we show that magnetic encapsulation by CrGeTe$_3$ achieves this in rhombohedral pentalayer graphene, by using switchable exchange couplings induced by ferromagnetic substrates. We further show that upon doping of the flat bands, spin triplet $f$-wave superconducting solutions emerge, whose layer and spin selectivity is controlled by the magnetic encapsulation, hosting in-gap excitations at the edges. Our results establish magnetic alignment as a means to control spin symmetry breaking and unconventional superconductivity in rhombohedral pentalayer graphene.

Abstract: We propose and analyze a three-terminal superconducting transistor switch in which a gate-defined quantum dot region interrupts a flux-controlled loop. The output supercurrent is switched off completely when the two loop arms couple to the dot with equal tunneling strengths and half a flux quantum threads the loop. The device then operates as a symmetric SQUID at half flux. The condition follows from a single geometric factor $χ$, which reduces the three-terminal problem to an equivalent two-terminal Anderson impurity model and makes the switching mechanism independent of the Coulomb interaction, the dot level position, the total hybridization and temperature. These enter only through a prefactor and thus decide how tolerant the device is to imperfect barrier tuning. Numerical renormalization group calculations across the full coupling parameter space show that the resonance is embedded in a broad region of elevated switching ratio at intermediate hybridization $Γ\approx Δ$, while both weaker and stronger coupling reduce it to a thin isolated line. Finite temperature smooths the underlying singlet-doublet transition and lowers the supercurrent amplitude, but affects the open and closed states comparably and leaves the switching ratio largely intact. Within a zero-bandwidth two-level model, a second dot level is shown to open a leakage channel that grows as the two levels approach in energy. Raising the output barrier and the realistic presence of inter-level Coulomb repulsion, however, suppress the leakage.

Abstract: Tensor network methods have been instrumental in addressing exponentially large many-body problems. Most recently, these techniques have also been applied to single-particle tight-binding Hamiltonians, allowing observables to be evaluated on exponentially large lattices. Here, we show that the scale-separated nature of these methods allows for a natural encoding of self-similar structures, such as the Sierpiński gasket, a scale-invariant fractal with Hausdorff dimension $\log 3/\log 2 \approx 1.58$, yielding an exact and compact representation of Hamiltonians on such lattices. Using this encoding, we study a Chern insulator, the flux kagome model, confined to this fractal geometry, and develop methods to investigate its topological states within a tensor network framework. This allows us to reach much higher fractal generations than would otherwise be possible with dense-matrix methods, resolving the density of states of a deep fractal with over 100 million sites. Furthermore, the larger structures allow us to resolve topology in smaller gaps, suggesting that these methods could be instrumental in understanding the interplay between topology and non-integer dimensionality.

Abstract: Symmetries that anticommute with a Hamiltonian can protect zero modes by obstructing their removal through symmetry-compatible perturbations. The residual and induced graded modules have isomorphic graded commutants, and their represented symmetry algebras share a graded Morita class that is super opposite to that of the commutant. We derive character criteria that identify these types. Exhaustive enumeration of the restriction relations gives independent sector contributions $0$, $Z$, or $Z_2$, with only integer factors in the purely unitary case. In the finite semisimple setting, the complete index vanishes if and only if all zero modes can be lifted by a symmetry-compatible perturbation. Applications to one-dimensional end states and interpolation cylinders of disordered boundary Dirac theories recover protected multiplet counts and connect the quotient to bulk topology and sigma-model topological terms using bulk--boundary correspondence, texture-index theorems, and fermion-phase calculations.

Abstract: Quantum communication networks are best supported by maximally entangled states, but imperfections in entanglement generation and distribution generally limit the distributed states to being only partially entangled. For a fixed query budget, generalized quantum signal processing (GQSP) restricts this response to a bounded trigonometric polynomial, while the Bayes classification error is linear in the response. These properties yield an exact finite-dimensional semidefinite program for the globally Bayes-optimal sensor response. Fejér--Riesz factorization and GQSP synthesis then recover a physically realizable control sequence. The same formulation extends naturally to sample-based design and additive phase uncertainty. Abstract: Programmable photonic circuits are a natural platform for physical learning, where hardware parameters are trained directly from measured feedback. We address this gap by introducing AI-driven optimization of quantum measurements on the incoming photons, learning how to extract the maximum possible information for a given computer vision task from each incoming photon. Motivation: D-正则 max-k-LINSAT 的低温平均情形是困难问题，其Gibbs分布采样（等价于经典自旋玻璃哈密顿量采样）在低温下难以高效完成。作者希望借助Regev基于量子傅里叶变换的归约，把该采样问题转化为译码问题，并寻求量子译码算法能达到的最低温，同时厘清该温度与经典采样困难性（重构阈值）之间的本质关系，从而给出量子优势的严格证据。

Method: 方法上，采用Regev归约把从特定自旋玻璃哈密顿量Gibbs分布抽样的问题转化为译码问题，并使用称为Belief Propagation with Quantum Messages (BPQM) 的量子置信传播译码算法求解该译码实例。理论分析上，证明Regev+BPQM可达到的最低温度恰等于树上信道传输信号时的重构阈值温度，并利用Renes关于经典信道与量子信道的对偶性加以解释：量子译码可行当且仅当对偶问题上的经典重构不可行。随后将重构温度与Mézard和Montanari所识别的1RSB腔方程预言的动力学玻璃转变温度对应起来，并在数百组问题参数上开展大规模数值实验，与Metropolis Monte Carlo、Glauber动力学等局部采样器的平衡行为进行对比。

Result: 主要结果有三：其一，严格证明Regev+BPQM可达最低温度等于树上噪声信道的重构阈值温度，并由Renes对偶给出其内在机制；其二，论证该重构温度正是1RSB腔方程给出的动力学玻璃转变温度；其三，数百组参数的大规模数值实验与“局部采样器在重构温度以下难以高效平衡、以上则可平衡”的猜想高度吻合。综合而言，Regev+BPQM达到了一个据作者所知不存在匹配经典保证的温度，即经典局部动力学高效Gibbs采样的猜想极限。

Conclusion: 本文把Regev归约与BPQM量子译码结合，给出了可达温度与信道重构阈值严格相等的结果，并借助信道对偶性揭示量子译码可行性与经典重构不可行性之间的等价。该温度对应1RSB动力学玻璃转变，数值实验支持经典局部采样在该温度以下的困难性。因此，Regev+BPQM在理论上达到经典局部动力学高效采样的猜想极限，为相关采样问题上量子算法相较经典局部方法的优势提供了严格与经验并重的证据。

Abstract: Regev's reduction uses a quantum Fourier transform to carry out reductions between various problems related to lattices and codes. We apply it to the problem of sampling from Gibbs distributions of certain classical spin glass Hamiltonians, which can equivalently be thought of as cost functions for average-case instances of an optimization problem called $D$-regular max-$k$-LINSAT. This problem becomes hard at low temperature. Here, we use a quantum decoding algorithm called Belief Propagation with Quantum Messages (BPQM) to solve the decoding problem arising from Regev's reduction. Our main contribution is a proof that the minimum temperature reachable using Regev+BPQM is exactly equal to the reconstruction threshold temperature for noisy channels transmitting signals on a tree network. This equality can be understood via Renes' duality between classical and quantum channels, which we use to show that quantum decoding is possible exactly when classical reconstruction on the dual problem is impossible. M{é}zard and Montanari identified this reconstruction temperature with a dynamical glass transition predicted by the one-step replica-symmetry breaking (1RSB) cavity equation. It is conjectured that local samplers such as Metropolis Monte Carlo and Glauber dynamics cannot equilibrate efficiently below this reconstruction temperature but may do so above it; our large numerical experiments show close agreement with this prediction across hundreds of different problem parameters. Therefore, our results rigorously show that Regev+BPQM reaches a temperature for which, to our knowledge, there is no matching classical guarantee, and brings Regev+BPQM to the conjectured limit of efficient Gibbs sampling by classical local dynamics.

</details>

Abstract: In differential atom interferometry, it is necessary to extract the differential phase shift between two interferometers that are individually noisy. A variety of techniques can be used for this purpose, under the general label of ellipse fitting. We compare several methods for ellipse fitting, considering parameters and performance relevant to atom interferometry. Statistical, algebraic, geometric and Bayesian methods are discussed, and we describe a novel "reciprocal" geometric method that offers some benefits. Each method has advantages and disadvantages that depend on the use case required. Supplemental material includes basic Python and Matlab code for each method.

Abstract: Superconducting circuits hosting long-lived bosonic modes are an excellent platform for emulating the dynamics of light and matter and exploiting them for quantum information processing. The space of accessible dynamics is greatly extended by introducing the frequency tunability of artificial atoms, whereby the interactions with the light field of a microwave cavity can be modified on demand. However, using external flux bias to control the frequency of transmons introduces significant losses to coupled cavities, reducing their single-photon lifetimes to a few tens of $μ$s. These imperfections compromise the usefulness of the bosonic mode, which loses its ability to store and manipulate the quantum information of many-photon states. This thesis investigates the issue and demonstrates how to enhance the flux control of microwave cavities without compromising protection. Several hardware solutions were explored, including off-plane remote flux sources routed by a magnetic hose, and lithographically printed on-chip lines equipped with multiple stages of filtering. These developments equip the systems with broadband flux control and photon lifetimes of several hundred $μ$s. They enable the real-time manipulation of the two central parameters of the Rabi model: detuning and coupling strength. This flexibility translates into operational primitives of interest, such as the calibration of nonlinearities to different interaction regimes, the activation of parametric resonances with adjustable coupling strengths, and the use of number-selective transmon-cavity transitions for qudit state preparation and control. These results lay the groundwork for exploring richer synthetic light-matter systems, controlling cavities with more complex nonlinear circuits, and engineering driven-dissipative systems with applications in quantum computation and sensing.

Conclusion: 该自适应协议通过有效利用有限测量预算，促进有限尺寸、采样受限量子实验中Rényi熵的高效估计。

Abstract: The second-order Rényi entropy is one of the most important quantities used for characterizing the entanglement structure of states prepared in quantum many-body experiments. With access to qubit projective measurement data, it can be estimated via a protocol in which repeated measurements are taken after each of a set of random local unitary operations is applied to the quantum state. To obtain accurate values for the second Rényi entropy on shot-limited devices, care must be taken when choosing the number of different unitary settings to apply, and the respective number of projective measurements to take. In this paper, we propose an adaptive protocol for estimating the Rényi entropy that iteratively optimizes the ratio of basis settings to measurements, using a gradient-based approach and starting from an initial small set of measurements. Using synthetic data, we demonstrate that for several prototypical states, our method converges to values for this ratio that are comparable to the optimal values obtained by a brute-force grid search, and it exhibits slower growth of the measurement budget required for a fixed target accuracy than fixed-ratio protocols. Our adaptive protocol therefore promotes efficient estimation of the Rényi entropy in finite-size, shot-limited quantum experiments by making effective use of the available measurement budget.

</details>

Abstract: Simulating high dimensional unstructured Partial Differential Equations (PDEs) with quantum processors is a major challenge with significant potential benefit. Existing methods have been demonstrated at small qubit counts, on simplifications of realistic problems. We implement a quantum reservoir network (QRN) for forecasting high-dimensional unstructured PDEs using up to 52 qubits on IBM superconducting hardware. Our method is gradient-free and does not suffer from the challenges of variational quantum algorithms. Instead, the QRN provides a large kernel space, up to 2^52 dimensions and beyond the reach of exact classical simulation, that remains useful under current hardware noise levels. Furthermore, a partial measure-and-reset mechanism enables total circuit runtime for ~24 times the qubit coherence time, so qubit lifetimes do not limit the forecast horizon. Thus, our method enables forecasting realistic unstructured nonlinear PDEs on quantum hardware. Typical difficulties associated with predicting large scale PDEs, such as transpilation, exponential shot requirements, hardware noise, and short qubit lifetimes are mitigated through careful design considerations and a dynamic observable reconstruction method. We validate our approach using a ~34k cell unstructured 2D cylinder flow at Reynolds number 1000 and a challenging nonlinear Gray--Scott reaction--diffusion problem, demonstrating high fidelity prediction on CUDA-Q simulation and on 26 and 52-qubit superconducting quantum processors. Specifically, we show that adding QRN features to an autoencoder linear prediction pipeline reduces prediction error by 21%-43%. Finally, we demonstrate closed-loop autoregressive PDE forecasting on these problems. This work demonstrates, for the first time, quantum circuits with depth beyond qubit lifetimes achieving accurate unstructured mesh forecasting on highly nonlinear PDEs.

</details>

*Seth Douglas,Nidhal Mghirbi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 证明有限非局域博弈中"完美正常空间策略"的存在性是Σ⁰₂-完全的，即使在博弈值为1时亦如此，故连停机神谕也无法判定；这比有限维模型(Σ⁰₁-完全)与对易算子模型(Π⁰₁-完全)中的完美博弈严格更难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画不同量子策略模型（有限维、对易算子、空间/张量积）下有限非局域博弈完美取胜的可判定性与计算复杂度，厘清空间模型与对易模型的本质差别。

Method: 可计算性理论（算术层级归约）分析：对空间模型、对易模型及带有限/半有限von Neumann宿主、III_λ型子因子宿主的完美正常策略构造归约；用迹(有限时钟周期/有限树深)作为存在量词代价；对分形阶α的有限熵条件Tr h^α<∞逐点刻画；并用Lean对相关结论进行形式化验证。

Result: 完美正常空间策略存在性为Σ⁰₂-完全（值1时亦然）；对易模型中带有限或半有限von Neumann宿主亦为Σ⁰₂-完全，而固定III_λ型子类型等同于无限制对易博弈的Π⁰₁-完全语言；迹恰消耗一个存在数词量词；有限纠缠熵要求下仍Σ⁰₂-完全；对任意可计算0<α<1，Tr h^α<∞的完美空间博弈为Σ⁰₂-完全，构成有限秩到有限熵的严格阶梯；一次性零误差通信（正常纠缠）亦Σ⁰₂-完全，并构造出给定码字规模却需无限纠缠熵的有限信道。

Conclusion: 量子策略模型的算力层级由宿主代数与熵/秩条件精确刻画：空间模型比有限维与对易模型严格更难，迹与有限熵各对应一个存在量词层级，且结果经Lean形式化验证。

Abstract: Given a finite nonlocal game, can the players win with certainty? We prove that the existence of a perfect normal spatial strategy is $Σ^0_2$-complete, even when restricted to games of value one, so it cannot be decided even with an oracle for the halting problem. This is strictly harder than perfect play in the finite-dimensional and commuting-operator models, which are $Σ^0_1$-complete and $Π^0_1$-complete. Within the commuting model, the existence of a perfect normal strategy with a finite or semifinite von Neumann host is $Σ^0_2$-complete, whereas for every fixed type $\mathrm{III}_λ$ subtype it has the same $Π^0_1$-complete language as unrestricted commuting play. A trace thus costs one existential number quantifier: the hardness constructions force every perfect strategy to choose a finite clock period or a finite tree depth. Spatial $Σ^0_2$-completeness also holds when finite entanglement entropy is required, and both forms are formally verified in Lean. For every computable $0<α<1$, perfect spatial play with $\mathrm{Tr}\,h^α<\infty$ for the reduced density $h$ is $Σ^0_2$-complete as well; these conditions form a strict ladder between finite rank and finite entropy. We also prove $Σ^0_2$-completeness for one-shot zero-error communication with normal entanglement, and construct a finite channel with zero-error codes of a given size each of which needs infinite entanglement entropy.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 纠缠输入下上述估计仍可经典高效完成，故此类应用不构成量子优势来源；但所有保证均为加性，既不提供指数小概率的相对估计，也不构成输出分布的采样器。

Abstract: Most proposed applications of boson samplers, including photonic machine-learning models, do not use the samples themselves but estimates of output probabilities, marginals or overlaps, obtained to additive precision from a finite number of shots. For product inputs such as single photons, these quantities can already be estimated classically with Gurvits's algorithm and its generalisations. We ask whether entangled inputs change this picture. For any $n$-photon input that is a matrix-product state of bond dimension $χ$, with one photon per site and $L$ modes per site, we give an unbiased estimator of any output amplitude of an $m$-mode interferometer, whose mean-square error after $N$ draws is at most $\|ψ\|^2/N$ whatever the interferometer, the output pattern and the entanglement of the state. Each draw costs $O(nLχ^2+nL^3+mn)$ operations after $O(nLχ^3)$ preprocessing, and an additive error $\varepsilon$ with probability at least $1-δ$ requires $O(\|ψ\|^2\varepsilon^{-2}\log(1/δ))$ draws, with memory of the order of the input size. The main application is to light emitted sequentially by quantum emitters, such as spin-based sources of photonic cluster states, for which $χ$ is bounded by the dimension of the source's retained quantum memory: one spin gives $χ=2$ at any length, and a memory of $k$ qubits, held in emitters or in photons stored in a delay line, gives $χ\le2^k$, so the cost stays polynomial as long as $k=O(\log n)$. The same sampler also estimates traced-memory probabilities, overlaps and individual marginal probabilities. All guarantees are additive: the estimator gives neither relative estimates of exponentially small probabilities nor a sampler of the output distribution.

</details>

Abstract: The variational quantum eigensolver (VQE) remains a cornerstone hybrid quantum-classical algorithm for quantum simulation, yet its scalability is fundamentally constrained by heavy measurement overheads. While strategies such as variance-preserved shot reduction (VPSR) mitigates this cost, they suffer from convergence instability and optimization stagnation caused by finite-sample bias in limited pilot shots. To overcome this limitation, we introduce delta-corrected shot reduction (DCSR), an analytically enhanced shot allocation strategy that rigorously mitigates finite-sample instability. Utilizing the multivariate delta method, we derive explicit higher-order bias correction terms for the empirical reduction factor. Both theoretical analysis and numerical simulations reveal that incorporating higher-order statistical moments, such as skewness and kurtosis, provides the gradient resolution necessary to bypass noise-induced barren plateaus. DSCR significantly accelerates convergence compared to standard VPSR across diverse noise and resource regimes while matching or decreasing cumulative measurement costs, establishing a mathematically rigorous framework for variance-stabilized shot allocation on near-term hardware.

</details>


### [66] [QAOA Inside ADMM: Subproblem Wins Do Not Become Decomposition Wins at Matched Budgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10527)
*Mohammad Arif Hossain,Sanim Yousuf Fahim*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在匹配预算下将QAOA作为两分块ADMM的二值子问题求解器与经典求解器基准对比：最终读数上QAOA不敌随机搜索，按最佳采样计入则占优；无噪声仿真中转移角度深度2 QAOA精确解率达97.3%，但时间标度更差；ADMM二值步实为约化问题的近端步，无一家族使QAOA取得优于最佳经典求解器的ADMM结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有文献常以不匹配的评估预算比较量子与经典求解器，缺乏在ADMM等混合整数框架内、以子问题求解器身份进行的公平基准评测；本文旨在以统一评估计数约定检验QAOA相对经典启发式与SDP+LS的实际竞争力。

Method: 对混合二值问题采用两分块ADMM，将二值子问题交由QAOA求解，每个子问题给23次优化器评估；采用"最终读数"与"最佳采样"两种记分口径，并设置正确性门与假设检验日志；在无噪声12量子比特仿真中使用转移角度、深度2的QAOA，与四种最强局部启发式、SDP+LS及带重启禁忌搜索对比，同时分析ADMM二值步在约化问题上的近端步性质及惩罚参数影响。

Result: 最终读数口径下，n=12与16时优化后的QAOA败于随机搜索（n=20不确定，属探索性事后检验）；按最佳采样计分则QAOA胜出。12量子比特仿真中深度2转移角度QAOA精确求得planted族97.3%的子问题最优，四种最强局部启发式为50.6%–67.4%（按最佳4个比特串计分则100%对90.4%–94.4%），SDP+LS为96.5%（差异不显著）。按评估计数约定，其求解时间比局部启发式低3–5倍但增长指数更大，为SDP+LS的10倍，以秒计时为带重启禁忌搜索的54倍。

Conclusion: 该基准类实为伪装的無约束二值二次问题，ADMM二值步等价于约化问题上的近端步：单位惩罚下ADMM重新求解该问题，惩罚增大时精确解愈发停滞；在两个不可约化族上，较不精确的求解器反而更常找到问题最优。没有任何族使QAOA获得优于最佳经典求解器与惩罚组合的ADMM结果，表明QAOA作为ADMM子问题求解器尚无实证优势。

Abstract: We benchmark the quantum approximate optimization algorithm (QAOA) as the binary subproblem solver in a two-block alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM) for mixed-binary problems against classical solvers under matched budgets. With 23 optimizer evaluations per subproblem, scoring determines the comparison: on its final readout, the optimized QAOA loses to random search at $n=12$ and 16 binary variables (inconclusive at 20; exploratory and post hoc tests). When credited with its best sample, it wins. In a noiseless 12-qubit simulation, transferred-angle depth-2 QAOA finds the exact optimum of 97.3\% of planted-family subproblems, against 50.6\%--67.4\% for the four strongest local heuristics (100\% against 90.4\%--94.4\% crediting the four best bitstrings) and a not significantly different 96.5\% for semidefinite rounding with local search (SDP+LS). Under our evaluation-counting convention, its time to solution there is a factor of three to five below the local heuristics' but grows with a larger exponent; it is ten times that of SDP+LS and, in seconds, 54 times that of tabu search with restarts. For the benchmark class, an unconstrained binary quadratic problem in disguise, ADMM's binary step is a proximal step on the reduced problem: at unit penalty ADMM re-solves it, and for larger penalties exact solutions increasingly stall. On two families that do not reduce to one, less accurate solvers found the problem optimum more often at unit and larger penalties. No family gave QAOA a better ADMM outcome than the best classical solver and penalty. The benchmark includes a correctness gate and a log of its hypothesis tests.

</details>


### [67] [Beyond the Mean Gap: What Transferred-Angle QAOA Retains on a Superconducting Processor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10529)
*Sanim Yousuf Fahim,Mohammad Arif Hossain*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文指出QAOA硬件验证常用"平均代价"对比存在盲区，在全局去极化噪声下应改为考察"最优解保留率"。在IBM Heron处理器上用深度2、迁移角度QAOA测试后，发现平均gap改善保留29%–46%，但最优比特串概率仅保留5%–15%，且首次投稿的器件匹配噪声模型高估最优保留5–8倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有QAOA硬件实验多以设备平均代价与模拟对比来验证，但该指标可能掩盖对优化真正重要的信息（是否保留最优解与近优解）。作者希望在可控去极化噪声下给出可检验的预测，并揭示平均指标与最优解指标之间的系统性偏差。

Method: 理论层面分析全局去极化信道：均值归一化gap与各比特串集合（含最优）经floor校正的概率，均保留相同的"相对均匀采样的改进比例"。实验层面在IBM Heron（ibm_marrakesh）上运行深度2、迁移角度的QAOA电路，求解交替方向分解的二元子问题，分别开启/关闭动态解耦与twirling；并在12比特主族与"rugged planted"族上做多实例、768-shot分块统计，同时构建器件匹配噪声模型作对照。

Result: 12比特主族电路平均gap改进保留29%–46%，但最优解概率仅保留5%–15%，每轮至少10个实例中有9个更低；近优比特串的损失与最优解相当，而"避免劣解"保留得最好。首次投稿的器件匹配噪声模型复现了模式，却将最优保留高估5–8倍。在rugged planted族上，设备在62%（768-shot块）中找到最优解（无噪声为98%），处于局部启发式方法的范围内。

Conclusion: 仅报告平均代价会高估QAOA硬件的优化能力。优化电路的验证应同时报告最优解及近优解集合的保留率，而不只是均值指标；器件匹配噪声模型在最优解保留的定量预测上不可靠。

Abstract: Hardware executions of the quantum approximate optimization algorithm (QAOA) are often validated by comparing the device's mean cost with a simulation, a check that can miss what matters for optimization. Under global depolarization, the mean normalized gap and the floor-corrected probability of every set of bitstrings, the optimum included, retain the same fraction of their noiseless improvement over uniform sampling. We test this prediction with depth-2, transferred-angle QAOA circuits for binary subproblems of an alternating-direction decomposition on the IBM Heron processor \texttt{ibm\_marrakesh}, with and without dynamical decoupling and twirling. At 12 qubits, the main-family circuits retained 29\% to 46\% of their mean-gap improvement but only 5\% to 15\% of their probability of the optimum, lower on at least nine of ten instances per run. Near-optimal bitstrings were lost about as much as the optimum, whereas the avoidance of poor bitstrings was retained best. In the first submission, a device-matched noise model reproduced the pattern but overestimated the retention of the optimum five- to eightfold. On a rugged planted family, the device found the optimum in 62\% of 768-shot blocks (noiseless: 98\%), within the range of local heuristics. Validations of optimization circuits should report retention of the optimum and of near-optimal sets.

</details>


### [68] [Compact Quantum Channel Attention for Static ASL Recognition: A Controlled Study with Parameter-Matched Baselines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10544)
*Ronak Bhalgami,Deep Kothadiya,Nikita Bhatt*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出 QCA-DenseNet：用8量子比特变分电路作为DenseNet121特征上的通道注意力门，在未见签名者上达94.50%均值准确率，与SE块及经典MLP无显著差异，仅体现参数效率优势，未发现量子优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合量子-经典模型常报告良好图像任务精度，但量子部分的实际贡献往往不明确；本文旨在澄清量子模块是否真正带来收益，并检验能否以更少参数实现等效的注意力机制。

Method: 构建静态ASL识别流水线：以八量子比特变分电路实现量子通道注意力（QCA）门，对DenseNet121特征做通道加权；QCA门共17,464参数，比SE块少7.51倍。在MU HandImages的1,815张图像上采用五折留一签名者交叉验证；并冻结骨干网络，在Kaggle ASL Alphabet小样本子集上做消融。对照设置包括SE块、参数匹配的经典MLP、无注意力模型，并做显著性检验。

Result: 未见签名者条件下，QCA-DenseNet平均准确率94.50%，高于SE的93.72%，与最优模型相差0.28个百分点；参数匹配的经典MLP与无注意力模型表现接近，各差异统计上均不显著。

Conclusion: QCA可在不产生可检测精度损失的前提下显著缩小注意力门参数规模，但未观察到量子优势；量子组件的作用更多体现为参数效率而非性能增益。

Abstract: Hybrid quantum-classical models often report good accuracy on image tasks, but it is rarely clear whether the quantum part is responsible. This paper proposes QCA-DenseNet, a static American Sign Language (ASL) recognition pipeline in which an eight-qubit variational circuit acts as a channel-attention gate on DenseNet121 features. The quantum channel attention (QCA) gate has 17,464 parameters, 7.51 times fewer than a squeeze-and-excitation (SE) block. The pipeline is tested on 1,815 MU HandImages images by holding out one of five signers at a time, and a frozen-backbone ablation uses small parts of the Kaggle ASL Alphabet data. On unseen signers, QCA-DenseNet reaches 94.50% mean accuracy, above SE (93.72%) and within 0.28 points of the best model. A matched classical MLP and a model without attention perform about the same, and no difference is significant. QCA gives a much smaller attention gate without a detectable loss in accuracy, but no quantum advantage.

</details>


### [69] [Initialization masks overcome decoding obstructions in compressed QAOA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10588)
*Emilio Semre,Steven Frankel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文揭示压缩量子优化中"解码非对易可观测量"这一步引入的结构性限制，指出现有 all-plus 初始化即使增加电路深度也无法突破，而改变初始化（有符号乘积态掩码）可在深度一即可获得可证明的加性优势，并用数值实验区分"解码表达能力"与"优化能量/实测性能"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 压缩量子优化需将变分量子态解码为经典指派，该解码步骤可能对可达目标值施加结构性约束；作者希望说明这类限制来源于初始化而非电路深度，并澄清解码表达能力、优化后能量与实测表现三者的区别。

Method: 构造 n=3r 顶点的连通三次图无限族，固定 Pauli 编码与确定性符号解码器，证明任意有限深度、任意实角序列的 all-plus QAOA 割值 ≤ 2r；提出固定深度一角下的均匀有符号乘积态掩码（期望割 13r/4，加性优势 5n/12），并给出存在最优掩码的无限子族；另构建在弱加权耦合、有界代价-角度域和指定编码器排序下仍保持线性深度一分离的第二构造；最后在 1,699 个随机三次 MaxCut 实例及加权 MaxCut、稀疏 Ising、Max-2-XOR、Max-2-SAT 完整队列上做无噪声仿真，用有限池次序统计量分离"选择效应"与"随机抽样效应"。

Result: 理论上 all-plus QAOA 割值上限 2r 与掩码期望 13r/4 形成深度无关的线性分离；数值上 1,699 个随机三次 MaxCut 实例中单一选定掩码平均比 0.9100，优于 all-plus X 基线 0.7067，并与 r² 个分别优化随机掩码的最优值 0.9125 接近；其他任务队列上选定掩码在各规模均优于基线，但小规模落后于掩码池，仅在较大 MaxCut 与 Ising 规模上显著领先。

Conclusion: 解码带来的表达性限制可通过初始化而非深度消除；论文区分了解码表达能力、优化能量与经验性能，且明确不主张量子计算优势。

Abstract: In compressed quantum optimization, a variational state is converted into a classical assignment by decoding noncommuting observables. We show that this step creates structural restrictions that changing the initialization can remove, even when increasing circuit depth cannot. For an explicit infinite family of connected cubic graphs on $n=3r$ vertices, with a fixed Pauli encoding and deterministic sign decoder, every all-plus QAOA circuit has cut at most $2r$, at every finite depth and every real angle sequence. A uniform signed product-state mask at fixed depth-one angles has expected cut $13r/4$, an additive advantage $5n/12$; an infinite subfamily admits an optimal mask. A second construction retains a linear depth-one separation under weak weighted couplings, a bounded cost-angle domain and a specified production-encoder ordering. Noiseless simulations then test classical mask selection before energy optimization. On 1,699 random cubic MaxCut instances, one selected mask achieves mean ratio $0.9100$, versus $0.7067$ for the all-plus $X$ baseline and $0.9125$ for the best of $r^2$ separately optimized random masks. On complete cohorts of weighted MaxCut, sparse Ising, Max-2-XOR and Max-2-SAT, the selected mask also improves on the baseline at every size, but it trails the pool at small sizes and leads it significantly only at the larger MaxCut and Ising sizes. Finite-pool order statistics separate the effect of selection from that of drawing a random mask. The results distinguish decoded expressivity from optimized energy and empirical performance; they do not establish a quantum computational advantage.

</details>


### [70] [Simulating the quantum switch with quantum circuits is impossible](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10615)
*Caisheng Cheng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明：任何具有经典受控因果序的量子电路，无论调用输入信道多少次（任意有限次），都无法精确且确定性地模拟量子开关；该不可能性对量子比特信道即已成立，并推广至完整 r-switch。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子开关是一种以不定因果序组合输入信道的二阶变换，其是否可被量子电路模拟、以及模拟需要多少次信道调用，是刻画"因果序量子控制能力"的自然问题；此前该问题缺乏关于精确确定性模拟的定论。

Method: 理论证明（不可能性定理）：针对具有经典受控因果序的量子电路这一类模拟模型，论证其对任意输入信道不可能以有限次调用精确、确定性地复现量子开关的作用；并将结论从两信道量子开关推广到相干控制 r 个信道全部置换的完整 r-switch。

Result: 证明了不存在任何有限调用次数的经典受控因果序量子电路能精确确定性地模拟量子开关；该结论对量子比特信道这一最简情形即成立，并延伸至完整 r-switch。

Conclusion: 解决了量子开关精确确定性电路模拟的问题：在该设定下，具有不定因果序的过程一般不能被经典受控因果序量子电路高效（乃至有限次）模拟，凸显了不定因果序作为一种独立于经典受控因果序的资源性能力。

Abstract: The quantum switch is a higher-order transformation that combines input quantum channels in an indefinite causal order. Whether quantum circuits can simulate its action, and how many calls to the input channels such a simulation requires, are natural questions about the power of quantum control of causal order. In this work, we prove that no quantum circuit with classically controlled causal order can exactly and deterministically simulate the quantum switch for arbitrary input channels using any finite number of calls. This impossibility holds already for qubit channels and extends to the complete $r$-switch, which coherently controls all permutations of $r$ input channels. These results settle the question of exact deterministic circuit simulation of the quantum switch. They demonstrate that processes with indefinite causal order cannot, in general, be efficiently simulated in this setting even by quantum circuits with classically controlled causal order.

</details>


### [71] [A Grover-Amplified Oracle for Maximum Clique on Graph, Minimum Hitting set and Dualizations on Hypergraphs Built on Quantum Phase Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10640)
*Francisco Piñero,Fernando L. \Pelayo,Hernan I. de la Cruz,Jose J. Paulet,Mauro Mezzini,Fernando Cuartero*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出图编码算子与超图编码算子两类量子线路，分别生成适用于Grover放大的最大团与最小命中集oracle，空间复杂度仅为O(N)量子位，并在模拟器上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Grover搜索的量子算法在求解最大团、最小命中集（及正布尔函数对偶化）时，量子位（空间）开销较大，尤其当超图边数随顶点数指数增长时更为突出。作者希望构造一种编码算子，使得oracle仅需与顶点数线性相关的量子位，从而在空间复杂度上优于已有方法。

Method: 1) 构造Graph Encoding Operator：作用于图G的补图Ḡ，输出最大团问题的oracle，可直接用于Grover振幅放大；2) 构造Hypergraph Encoding Operator：作用于超图H，输出最小命中集问题的oracle；3) 给出两种算子的完整线路构造并证明其正确性；4) 分析时间复杂度与空间复杂度；5) 在量子模拟器上实现验证；6) 测得最小命中集oracle可近似估计命中集数目，从而求解以正不可冗余析取范式表示的正布尔函数对偶化问题。

Result: 两类算子均正确生成可供Grover放大的oracle，并在量子模拟器上完成实现。空间复杂度方面，编码及对应oracle仅需O(N)个量子位（N为顶点数），即使超图含有指数级数量的超边亦如此。与现有基于Grover搜索的量子算法相比，在空间复杂度上（据作者所知）优于此前所有算法；最小命中集oracle测量结果还可给出超图命中集数量的近似，从而解决相应正布尔函数的对偶化问题。

Conclusion: 通过图/超图编码算子可将最大团与最小命中集问题统一为可用Grover放大求解的oracle形式，并以O(N)量子位实现显著的量子位节省；该框架还顺带给出命中集计数近似与正布尔函数对偶化的量子解法。作者认为其算法在空间复杂度上优于现有方法。

Abstract: In this paper, we use a quantum circuit called the Graph Encoding Operator which, applied to the complement $\bar G$ of any graph $G$, yields an oracle for the maximum clique problem suitable for amplification via Grover's algorithm. Analogously, we devise a Hypergraph Encoding Operator which, when applied to a hypergraph $\mathcal H$, yields an oracle for the minimum hitting set suitable for amplification via Grover's algorithm. Interestingly, the latter oracle provides, when measured, an approximation of the number of hitting sets of the hypergraph, thus solving the dualization problem for positive Boolean functions represented in a positive irredundant disjunctive normal form. We give the full construction of both operators, prove their correctness, analyze their complexity, and provide an implementation on a quantum simulator. We discuss the resulting complexity, contrast them with existing quantum computer algorithms based on Grover search and we will show that our algorithms outperform, to the best of our knowledge, all of the previous algorithms on space computational complexity by requiring only $O(N)$ number of qubits with respect to the number of vertices $N$, for representing both graphs and hypergraphs in the encoding operator and, consequently, results in an oracle for both graphs and hypergraphs that uses only $O(N)$ qubits even when the hypergraph contains an exponential (on the number of vertices) number of hyperedges.

</details>


### [72] [Demonstration of a partial knowledge quantum key distribution protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10670)
*Liran Shirizly,Leonid Vidro,Hagai S. Eisenberg*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出将 SARG04 推广到 d 维、q 基的 QKD 协议 Bridge[d,q]，并实验演示 Bridge[2,3]，仅用单个普克尔斯盒完成三种无偏偏振基切换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SARG04 类协议在部分信息条件下仍可提取密钥，其维度与基数目可推广；同时 3 基 QKD 需要快速、稳定的偏振基切换手段。

Method: 构造 Bridge[d,q] 协议：Alice 与 Bob 仅获知对方原始密钥的部分信息，但该部分信息足以提炼确定的共享加密密钥；实验上以单个 Pockels cell 实现三种无偏偏振基的高速切换，演示 Bridge[2,3]。

Result: 实验实现了 Bridge[2,3] 协议，验证了基于单普克尔斯盒的三基偏振切换方案可行。

Conclusion: Bridge[d,q] 为 SARG04 的维度/基数目推广，部分信息足以生成共享密钥；单普克尔斯盒偏振基切换方法可推广至任意 3 基 QKD 协议及需快速操控偏振量子比特的应用。

Abstract: We demonstrate a novel QKD protocol, named \texttt{Bridge[$d,q$]}, which generalizes the \texttt{SARG04} protocol to $d$ dimensions and $q$ bases. In this protocol, Alice and Bob gain only partial knowledge about each other's raw key. Nevertheless, this partial information is sufficient to distill a definite mutual encryption key. We present an experimental demonstration of the \texttt{Bridge[$2,3$]} protocol. The three unbiased polarization bases were switched using a single Pockels cell. The applied polarization basis switching method is relevant to any 3-basis QKD protocol and to other applications that require fast manipulation of photon polarization qubits.

</details>


### [73] [From Machine-Discovered Graphs to Analytic NOON State Families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10685)
*Ozra Mahmoudi,Fatemeh Ahmadi,Rahman Nouroozi*

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Parameterized instantaneous quantum polynomial-time (IQP) circuits generate distributions conjectured to be hard to sample classically. As reinforcement learning (RL) policies, their $2^n$ measurement outcomes are typically mapped to a smaller action space by a classical decoder. This aggregation can mitigate initialization concentration while making the policy classically simulable. We characterize this tradeoff through the decoder's Fourier--Pauli expansion. Under independent uniform initialization, we express policy variance as an exact sum over groups of nonconstant Pauli components with identical active-generator signatures. Each group contributes its squared aggregate coefficient times $2^{-r}$, where $r$ is the binary rank of its active generators. An inverse-polynomial aggregate coefficient with logarithmic rank prevents exponential policy concentration. Sparse Pauli expansions and contiguous action bins permit efficient classical additive estimation of action probabilities. We introduce a majority decoder combining protected low-weight components with exponentially large exact Fourier support, preserving initialization nonconcentration while excluding polynomial-size enumeration of the exact expansion. Numerical experiments corroborate the variance laws. In ten-qubit CartPole experiments, majority achieves higher returns than recursive parity and the tested local readouts, while higher-weight Pauli sectors contribute an increasing relative share of gradient magnitude during training. A complementary 21-qubit block-chain policy outperforms an extensive pool of fully connected classical neural networks in episode efficiency, even though its bounded-width structure permits efficient classical simulation. These results guide decoder design for quantum and quantum-inspired RL, distinguishing policy nonconcentration, learning performance, and classical simulability.

</details>

Abstract: Telecom-wavelength quantum light sources are essential components for scalable fiber-based quantum networks, where on-demand emission of single photons is required for high secret key rates. Here, we demonstrate the deterministic integration of metal-organic vapor-phase epitaxy (MOVPE)-grown C-band InAs/InP quantum dots into circular Bragg grating resonators. The integrated emitters exhibit a radiative lifetime of $T_1 = (1400 \pm 50)~\mathrm{ps}$ and a spectral linewidth of $ΔE_{\mathrm{FWHM}} = (41.0 \pm 4.0)~μ\mathrm{eV}$. Under non-resonant excitation, we measure a post-selected single-photon purity of $(0.95 \pm 0.02)$ and a raw two-photon interference visibility of $V_{12\mathrm{ns}} = (35 \pm 2)\%$, which increases to $(70 \pm 18)\%$ under temporal post-selection. We discuss strategies to improve photon indistinguishability, including radiative-rate engineering, charge stabilization, and more selective excitation. Our results combine MOVPE growth, deterministic device integration, and telecom C-band emission, providing a route toward scalable semiconductor quantum light sources for fiber-based quantum networks.

Abstract: Proofs generated by large language models are often difficult to understand, generalize, or formalize directly. Here we verify, digest, and formalize OpenAI's computer-assisted proof of a uniform gap in the periodic spin-1 Heisenberg chain using tensor-network diagrams and Lean 4. Reading the partition-function network along space and imaginary time shows how finite-ring bounds propagate across doubled lengths and inverse temperatures to all longer even rings, sharpening the spin-1 bound from the same finite inputs by a factor of 19 to $Δ_L > 0.0729$ for all even $L \geq 36$. We identify reflection positivity as the general condition behind the proof and certify uniform gaps in seven additional spin chains and ladders. More broadly, digesting machine-generated proofs into transparent physical mechanisms yields sharper bounds, broader theorems, and reusable formal verifications.

  <summary>Details</summary>
