Method: 将SqDRIFT（基于随机采样与Krylov子空间的对角化）与解析核梯度耦合，构建SqDRIFT-AIMD工作流；在40量子比特活性空间计算基态能量与核力，并在IBM Heron r3量子处理器上执行电路，用于Zundel阳离子质子转移动力学模拟。

Abstract: Ab initio molecular dynamics (AIMD) requires the calculation of highly accurate electronic-structure energies and nuclear forces at every time step, which can quickly become computationally expensive. Sample-based quantum diagonalization offers a route to such calculations on quantum hardware, but its use in AIMD simulations has so far been limited to active spaces of about 20 qubits. In these spaces, exact diagonalization is tractable, and the quantum circuits are typically built from standard coupled-cluster amplitudes. Here, we introduce SqDRIFT-AIMD, which combines randomized sample-based Krylov quantum diagonalization (SqDRIFT) with analytical nuclear gradients. With SqDRIFT-AIMD, we obtain more accurate ground-state energies than the conventional coupled-cluster singles and doubles (CCSD) driven AIMD simulation for the $40$-qubit active space. We apply SqDRIFT-AIMD to study the dynamics of proton transfer in the Zundel cation, using circuits executed on IBM Heron r3 quantum processors. SqDRIFT energies fall below CCSD across the $40$-qubit double-well PES and stay within a few m$E_\text{h}$ of CCSD at 52 qubits. The $40$-qubit SqDRIFT-AIMD trajectory tracks the SHCI-driven proton transfer to within $1.33$ mÅ and exhibits excellent total energy conservation, with a standard deviation of only $0.245$ m$E_\text{h}$. These results extend quantum-centric AIMD to active spaces beyond the reach of exact diagonalization.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We develop a feedback-controlled quantum metrology scheme to probe weak gravitational waves (GWs) through their direct energy exchange with light. Adaptive SU(2) direction control, based on current estimates of the GW frequency and phase, allows the frequency shifts acquired in successive interaction intervals to add rather than cancel. The resulting persistent frequency difference is converted into an optical phase difference for interferometric readout. We further extend the scheme to elliptically polarized GWs using two transverse sensing channels separated by $45^\circ$ to probe the $+$ and $\times$ components independently. After the GW interaction, the two signals are mapped onto orthogonal polarization modes, enabling phase accumulation along a common path. We derive the corresponding quantum Fisher information matrix and find a $T_{\rm int}^2T_{\rm ph}^2$ scaling in the ideal coherent regime. For a fixed-photon-number probe, the sensitivity shows Heisenberg-like $1/n$ scaling in the ideal case.

</details>

Motivation: 核心问题：量子纠缠能否产生在纠缠本身消失后仍然持续的宏观有序？作者希望厘清"瞬态量子资源"与"持久宏观序"之间的因果关系，并为非平衡系统自组织中的量子-经典边界提供严格判据。

Method: 建立驱动-耗散玻色子晶格模型，其中局域纠缠促成的输运将空间涨落放大为扩展Turing斑图。针对含噪声与无序的全非线性动力学，推导适用于所有可分离演化（容许任意经典关联）的斑图强度有限时间严格上界；以超越该上界的"过量强度"作为超出可分离极限的累积输运贡献的量化下界。进一步引入对环境响应的机制：环境对涌现有序作出响应，抑制局域相干并激活经典输运，且两个阶段由同一时间无关Lindblad方程描述。

Result: 量子动力学在斑图形成与噪声下稳定两个阶段均产生超出可分离上界的斑图强度；过量强度同时给出累积输运贡献超越可分离极限的下界。在环境响应机制下，局域纠缠丧失后斑图仍作为宏观序持续存在，且两个阶段统一由单一时间无关Lindblad方程刻画。

Conclusion: 瞬态量子资源（纠缠）可建立持久的宏观有序，说明纠缠可作为"催化剂"而非持续支撑者；结果为在非平衡系统中利用自组织、并区分量子与经典关联的作用提供了可操作判据与新方向。

Abstract: Can quantum entanglement generate macroscopic order that persists after the entanglement itself disappears? We establish such a mechanism in a driven-dissipative bosonic lattice, where local entanglement enables transport that amplifies spatial fluctuations into extended Turing patterns. For the full nonlinear dynamics with noise and disorder, we derive rigorous finite-time bounds on pattern intensity for all admissible separable evolutions, allowing arbitrary classical correlations. Quantum dynamics produces pattern intensities beyond these bounds during both formation and stabilization under noise. The excess intensity also provides a lower bound on the accumulated transport contribution beyond the separable limit. We further introduce an environment that responds to emerging order by suppressing local coherence and activating classical transport. The patterns then persist as local entanglement is lost, with both stages arising from a single time-independent Lindblad equation. These results show how transient quantum resources can establish persistent macroscopic order, opening new directions for harnessing self-organization in nonequilibrium systems.

</details>


### [82] [Hardware-Efficient Continuous-Variable Quantum Computation: Finite-$N$ Non-Gaussian Gates in a Floquet-Engineered Cavity-BEC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11245)
*Soi-Chan Lei*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 利用单腔87Rb BEC作为宏观玻色逻辑模式，通过Floquet工程设计确定性二轴扭转(TAT)非高斯门，并用精确MPO与Dicke投影张量网络方法模拟，证明过挤压区可确定性地产生Wigner负性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续变量(CV)玻色模式量子计算是通往容错的硬件高效路径，但确定性、宏观、带Wigner负性的非高斯门难以合成；同时现有数值方法受Fock截断误差和O(N^3)置换不变计算的限制。

Method: 以单腔87Rb BEC为宏观玻色逻辑模式，基于正交光双驱动架构提出无后选择的确定性幺正Floquet TAT门；用正规序Holstein-Primakoff展开与非微扰自旋模拟分析有限N Bloch球曲率(立方剪切与四次Kerr)；构造SU(2)自旋基下精确自动机MPO(键维D_L=6，与N无关)；用带log-gamma归一化的精确双边动态规划Dicke投影器，从4^N维Lindblad演化中无矩截断地提取(N+1)×(N+1)对称投影，并追踪对称性保持率p_sym(t)。

Result: 在N=50基准上，于t=2.00μs获得深Wigner负性W_min≈-0.214，此前在t_opt≈0.64μs处ξ_R^2≈0.124；方法规避N_max截断与O(N^3)标度。

Conclusion: 建立了可扩展的宏观BEC处理器架构，可推广至N~10^3–10^5。

Abstract: Continuous-variable (CV) quantum computation in bosonic modes offers a hardware-efficient route toward fault tolerance, yet synthesizing deterministic, macroscopic non-Gaussian gates with Wigner negativity remains challenging. Here, we establish two physical breakthroughs and two tensor-network innovations using a single-cavity 87Rb Bose-Einstein condensate (BEC) as a macroscopic bosonic logical mode. Physically, building on our orthogonal optical dual-driving architecture, we propose deterministic, unitary Floquet-engineered Two-Axis Twisting (TAT) non-Gaussian gates without post-selection, and introduce a paradigm shift from quantum metrology to CV quantum computation: via a normal-ordered Holstein-Primakoff expansion and non-perturbative spin simulations, we show that the over-squeezed regime ($t>t_{opt}$, where Wineland squeezing $ξ_R^2$ degrades) harnesses finite-$N$ Bloch-sphere curvature - manifesting at $O(1/S)$ as cubic shearing and quartic Kerr $(a^\dagger a)^2$ interactions - to deterministically generate Wigner negativity. Methodologically, we formulate an exact automaton Matrix Product Operator in the compact SU(2) spin basis with an $N$-independent Liouvillian bond dimension ($D_L=6$), bypassing Fock cutoff errors ($N_{max}$) and $O(N^3)$ permutational-invariant scaling. Finally, we decouple $4^N$-dimensional Lindblad evolution from collective observation via an Exact Bilateral Dynamic Programming Dicke Projector with log-gamma normalization, extracting the $(N+1)\times(N+1)$ symmetric projection $\tildeρ_{n_L,n_R}$ without moment truncation while tracking symmetry retention $p_{sym}(t)$. Our proof-of-principle $N=50$ benchmark resolves deep Wigner negativity ($W_{min} \approx -0.214$ at $t=2.00μ$s, after $ξ_R^2 \approx 0.124$ at $t_{opt} \approx 0.64μ$s), establishing a scalable architecture for $N \sim 10^3-10^5$ macroscopic BEC processors.

</details>


### [83] [Unbounded advantage of adaptivity in entanglement-assisted communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11333)
*Armin Tavakoli*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文引入自适应量子通信任务，展示仅需三个纠缠辅助消息即可完成、而非自适应协议通信代价无界；同时给出自适应与非自适应协议之间的两条互补限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共享纠缠可减少分布式计算所需经典通信量，既可先测量纠缠再经典通信，也可令量子测量依赖于收到的消息（自适应）。作者希望厘清自适应协议相对于非自适应协议在通信代价上的根本优势及其界限。

Method: 构造一类任务：自适应协议仅用三个纠缠辅助消息即可求解，而非自适应量子接收者所需通信代价随参数无界增长；并分析纠缠辅助三元经典信道所产生关联的可复现性，进而证明自适应与非自适应协议关系的两条限制性定理（有限纠缠维度下的有限代价非自适应模拟；d 个自适应消息可用 O(d log(1/ε)) 个非自适应消息以误差 ε 模拟）。

Result: 存在任务使自适应协议仅需三个纠缠辅助消息，而非自适应情形通信成本无界；因此任何有界的经典通信量都无法复现纠缠辅助三元经典信道生成的全部关联。同时证明：任何使用有限纠缠维度的自适应协议都可由有限通信代价的非自适应协议模拟；任何发送 d 个消息的自适应协议都可用 O(d log(1/ε)) 个非自适应消息在误差 ε 内模拟。

Conclusion: 自适应性在量子通信中具有本质上无界的优势，但观测到极大优势需要同时具备高维纠缠与极高精度的测量；自适应优势在有限纠缠维度或有限精度下均受可控约束。

Abstract: Shared entanglement can reduce the amount of classical communication needed to perform distributed computation. This can be achieved either by first measuring the entanglement and then communicating classically, or by adapting the quantum measurement to the received message. Here, we introduce tasks that adaptive protocols can solve using just three entanglement-assisted messages, while the communication cost grows without bound when the quantum receiver is not adaptive. Consequently, no bounded amount of classical communication can reproduce all correlations generated by an entanglement-assisted ternary classical channel. We establish two complementary limitations on the relation between adaptive and non-adaptive quantum protocols. First, every adaptive protocol using a finite entanglement dimension admits a non-adaptive simulation at finite communication cost. Second, every adaptive protocol sending $d$ messages can be simulated to within error $\varepsilon$ using $O(d\log(1/\varepsilon))$ non-adaptive messages. Our results reveal the fundamentally unbounded advantage of adaptivity in quantum communication, but also that observing very large advantages requires both high-dimensional entanglement and very precise observations.

</details>


### [84] [What an Observer Reads off a Holographic Screen](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11356)
*Kenan Oggad,James F. Glazebrook,Chris Fields*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文把"广义全息原理"中可分离系统间相互作用在全息屏上的表述抽象为带权重的交换可观测量加权和，用"profile"（权重表）刻画探针可读出的谱，证明"各权重下均携带profile"当且仅当量子比特的非零迹两两线性无关，并给出扩散探针维数不足、profinite/condensed 结构以及模糊球极点距离、上下文性与 ER=EPR 等应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望在"量子比特阵列式全息屏"而非连续曲面的情形下，厘清观测者究竟能读出什么：在广义全息原理中，两可分离系统的相互作用表现为屏上若干交换可观测量的加权和，因此需要系统刻画何种权重配置与何种量子比特组织方式能使读出谱只依赖于权重表，并回答"何时读出携带完整profile"以及分组细化极限下的结构问题。

Method: 1) 将屏上相互作用建模为交换可观测量（取值±1）的加权和乘总前因子，权重非负、前因子为1、联合本征空间为一维直线时，谱只依赖权重表（profile）。2) 引入扩散探针测量"running dimension"，在等权归一屏上给出一单位时间维数亏缺的界（≤2/比特数），并分析几何profile经层级计数产生的任意正高度平台。3) 将观测者读出定义为对量子比特集合的关联，用"迹"（包含该比特的集合列表）刻画；证明读出在所有权重下携带profile当且仅当不同非零迹在二元域上线性无关，此时读出在可逆重编码下是被覆盖比特的一个划分；依赖型读出仅在有限多个真线性子空间上携带profile。4) 对分组无限细化构造以profinite集（从而是condensed集）为边界的塔，讨论严格正相容profile对局部常值函数的分离距离。

Result: 等权归一屏上单位时间扩散维数亏缺不超过比特数倒数的两倍；几何profile可经层级计数产生任意正高度的平台。核心定理给出"全权重携带profile ⟺ 非零迹在F₂上线性无关"，并指出此类读出等价于（可逆重编码下的）划分，而依赖型读出只在有限多个真线性子空间上携带profile。无限细分给出profinite/condensed边界及正相容profile的分离距离。应用方面可认证概率性与强上下文性、给出模糊球极点间距的首阶亏缺，并证明在单一配偶纠缠模型中可同时被权重一线程占据的对恰为部分匹配。

Conclusion: 量子比特阵列屏上的可读谱在很一般的条件下确实由权重表决定，而"处处可读"是一种强约束，等价于迹的F₂线性无关性，即读出的划分结构；分组细化的极限自然落入condensed数学框架，并可导出上下文性认证与全息纠缠（ER=EPR）的匹配约束等结论。

Abstract: What does an observer read off a holographic screen that is an array of qubits rather than a surface? In the generalized holographic principle the interaction between two separable systems acts on such a screen as a weighted sum, up to an overall prefactor, of commuting observables with values plus and minus one. When the weights are nonnegative and the prefactor is one and the joint eigenspaces are lines, everything a probe reads off the spectrum depends only on the list of weights, which we call the profile. On the screen with equal weights summing to one the running dimension read by a diffusion probe falls short of two at unit time by at most twice the reciprocal of the number of qubits; a geometric profile gives instead, through its level count, a plateau of any positive height. An observer correlates sets of qubits; its readout carries a profile at a given weighting when the levels it reads, counted with multiplicity, are those of a screen with one weight per set. We prove that a readout carries a profile at every weighting exactly when the distinct nonzero traces of its qubits are linearly independent over the field with two elements, the trace of a qubit being the list of sets that contain it; such a readout is a partition of the qubits it covers up to an invertible recoding, and a dependent one carries a profile only on finitely many proper linear subspaces of weightings. Refining a grouping without end builds a tower whose boundary is a profinite set and hence a condensed set, on which a strictly positive compatible profile separates the locally constant functions by a distance. Applications certify probabilistic and strong contextuality and give the leading shortfall of the distance between the poles of the fuzzy sphere; in a model of monogamous entanglement the pairs that weight-one threads representing operational ER=EPR can occupy at once are exactly the partial matchings.

</details>


### [85] [Information--control limits for record-indistinguishable reset of a monitored qubit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11393)
*Sinan Bugu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究连续监测下量子比特的"擦除"问题：用反馈控制把激发态比特复位到基态，同时使其测量记录在统计上逼近未驱动基态比特的记录，并给出记录可区分度D与控制代价J之间的精确权衡关系及高代价极限常数K(η)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 被连续监测的激发态比特会把"制备历史"写入测量记录，因此单纯复位并不会抹除这份信息。作者要量化：在不改变记录统计分布的前提下完成复位，需要多少控制代价，以及信息泄漏（记录可区分度）与代价之间的根本下限。

Method: 模型为σ_z连续测量（速率Γ_m、效率η、无弛豫）。以因果反馈为容许控制，用Kullback–Leibler散度D刻画受控记录律与参考律的可区分度，用二次代价J（积分平方Rabi频率）刻画控制强度。证明开环驱动使D发散，并给出DJ≥Γ_m·U(η,D/κ_0)的权衡不等式；再通过两相反馈策略构造序列并求ε↓0的精确极限常数K(η)，同时用积分形式界定全局常数的上下界。

Result: 所有开环驱动都使D=∞；任何容许控制满足DJ≥Γ_m U(η,D/κ_0)，κ_0=1−ln2。核心结果：lim_{ε↓0} inf{DJ/Γ_m : D≤ε}=K(η)，其中K(η)=[∫_0^π √(2η(1+cosθ)²+(1−η)sin²θ) dθ]²，并由显式两相反馈策略达到。全局常数c(η)=inf DJ/Γ_m满足K/2≤F_2≤c≤K；η=1及η→0极限下c精确已知。

Conclusion: 记录可区分度与控制代价存在由测量效率η决定的基本下限，被积函数中的两项分别对应旋转过程中的布居证据与未记录测量部分退相干（去纯化）产生的"纯度代价"。c(η)仅适用于无弛豫模型，任何非零弛豫率都会使DJ的无约束下确界消失。

Abstract: A qubit that is continuously monitored from the moment it is prepared in its excited state writes evidence of that preparation into its measurement record. We ask how much control is needed to reset such a qubit to its ground state while keeping its complete record statistically close to the record of an undriven ground-state qubit. The qubit is measured in $σ_z$ at rate $Γ_m$ with efficiency $η$ and has no relaxation. For feedback that acts causally on the record, distinguishability is the Kullback--Leibler divergence $D$ of the controlled record law from the reference law, and the control cost is the quadratic effort $J$, the integrated squared Rabi frequency. We prove that every open-loop drive leaves $D$ infinite and that every admissible control obeys $DJ\geΓ_mU(η,D/κ_0)$, where $U$ is given by a one-parameter family of integrals and $κ_0=1-\ln2$. Our main result is the exact high-effort constant $\lim_{ε\downarrow0}\inf\{DJ/Γ_m: D\leε\}=K(η)$, where $K(η)=[\int_0^π\sqrt{2η(1+\cosθ)^2+(1-η)\sin^2θ}\,dθ]^2$; an explicit sequence of two-phase feedback policies attains it. The first term of the integrand is the population evidence that the record receives while the qubit is rotated toward the ground state. The second is the price of the impurity created when the unrecorded part of the measurement dephases the coherence that the rotation needs; the record receives this evidence later, when the measurement repurifies the qubit. The global constant $c(η)=\inf DJ/Γ_m$ satisfies $K/2\le F_2\le c\le K$, where $F_2$ is again given by an integral; $c$ is known exactly at $η=1$ and in the limit $η\to0$. With any nonzero relaxation rate the unconstrained infimum of $DJ$ vanishes, so $c(η)$ is specific to the relaxation-free model.

</details>

*Richard Bernecker,Baghdasar Baghdasaryan,Fabian Steinlechner,Stephan Fritzsche*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文通过泵浦光工程化，在SPDC产生的OAM模式中实现任意维度d的高维最大纠缠态（MES），并揭示泵浦空间结构与OAM子空间选择之间的相互作用决定了所需泵浦轮廓。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SPDC产生的双光子态虽具高维空间纠缠，但因OAM模式分布极不均匀而非最大纠缠；已有泵浦工程方法多局限于低维子空间，缺乏对任意维度d（d>2）最大纠缠态生成条件的普遍理解。

Method: 在d×d子空间内，分析空间泵浦结构与OAM子空间选择之间的相互作用，据此直接确定目标MES所需的泵浦轮廓，实现任意维度高维MES的生成。

Result: 证明泵浦工程可生成任意d维的d×d子空间高维MES，并给出由泵浦结构与子空间选择共同决定的目标泵浦轮廓设计规则。

Conclusion: 实现OAM最大纠缠需要对有效高维Hilbert子空间进行精细且非平凡的设计，子空间选择与泵浦整形同等重要。

Abstract: Spontaneous parametric down-conversion (SPDC) generates correlated photon pairs that inherently exhibit high-dimensional spatial entanglement in orbital angular momentum (OAM) modes. In practice, the resulting two-photon states are typically not maximally entangled due to a highly non-uniform distribution of generated OAM modes. Although pump-beam engineering has emerged as a tool to prepare high-dimensional entangled states, it has usually been restricted to low-dimensional subspaces, i.e., small sets of selected OAM modes for each photon within the full infinite-dimensional two-photon Hilbert space. A general understanding of the requirements for the generation of maximally entangled states (MESs) for any dimensionality $d$ with $d>2$ is lacking. Here, we demonstrate how pump engineering enables the generation of high-dimensional MESs for arbitrary $d$ defined in $d \times d$ subspaces. By identifying the interplay between the spatial pump structure and the choice of OAM subspace, the required pump profile for a target MES is directly determined. Our results reveal that maximal entanglement with OAM requires a careful and nontrivial design of the effective high-dimensional Hilbert subspace.

</details>


### [87] [Universal robust nonadiabatic geometric quantum computation with shortened composite pulses on superconducting circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11420)
*Cheng-Yun Ding,Na Kuang,Yu Jin,Li Tang,Rui Huang,Li-Hua Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种基于缩短复合脉冲的普适鲁棒非绝热几何量子门方案，以简单脉冲控制实现较短的等效演化并有效利用足够多的演化参数抑制普适系统误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统NGQC结合最优控制提升保真度与鲁棒性时，往往以延长门时间为代价，或需要复杂脉冲控制，且仅局限于特定量子门或单一误差源；因此，如何在进一步缩短门时间的同时，以简单控制实现普适且鲁棒的几何门仍是主要挑战。

Method: 采用缩短复合脉冲（shortened composite pulses）构造普适非绝热几何门；脉冲控制形式简单，并能提供足够多的可调演化参数，用于降低门对普适系统误差的敏感性。

Result: 数值模拟表明，该普适几何门集合相比传统单环对应方案具有显著更强的抗误差能力；并在实验技术已实现的真实超导量子电路上验证了该方案的鲁棒性优势。

Conclusion: 该工作为实现抗多重误差的高保真、鲁棒几何量子计算提供了一条可行替代途径。

Abstract: The nonadiabatic geometric quantum computation (NGQC) using the nonadiabatic geometric phases is a promising strategy to realize the high-fidelity quantum operation for large-scale quantum processing. However, conventional NQGC schemes combining with optimal control techniques to further improve the gate fidelity and robustness usually either comes at the cost of prolonged gate time or require complex pulse control and only are limited to several certain specific quantum gates or/and for a certain type error source. Thus, implementing universal and robust geometric gates with simple control while further suppressing gate times remains a major challenge. Here, we propose a scheme for universal robust geometric gates with shortened composite pulses, where the pulse control is simple and enough available evolution parameters can be utilized to effectively reduce the sensitivity to universal systematic errors. Numerical simulations demonstrate that our universal geometric gates set exhibit much stronger error resilience than their conventional single-loop counterparts. Finally, we also verify the robustness advantage of our scheme on realistic superconducting quantum circuits with experimentally demonstrated technology. Therefore, our work paves an alternative way to achieve high-fidelity and robust geometric quantum computation against multiple errors.

</details>


### [88] [Alchemically Transferable Parameters for Variational Quantum Eigensolvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11445)
*Jonas Motyl,Jakob S. Kottmann*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 通过"炼金术"式几何投影，将目标有机分子的局域成键环境映射到小型氢化物片段，从而复用已有的氢化物求解器，无需针对有机分子重新训练机器学习模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习预测变分量子本征求解器（VQE）的电路参数，可绕开经典优化循环中的贫瘠高原、局部极小及高昂能量/梯度评估瓶颈。此前工作证明，这种模型配合结构化电路设计可在氢化物体系内跨体系尺寸与结构迁移。然而将其扩展到有机分子，通常看似需要用例专属训练集重新训练，本文旨在消除这一需求。

Method: 提出一种炼金术（alchemical）方法：利用几何投影，沿目标分子的化学键方向放置替代氢原子，将目标分子的局域成键环境映射到小型氢化物片段上，从而原样复用已有氢化物求解器。唯一新增的可调量为每种键类型的单一投影半径，该半径在小参考分子上拟合一次后保持不变。在既有的可分离对（separable-pair）电路设计上验证，并将方法扩展到跨越量子子空间有效基的多电路方法。

Result: 在可分离对电路设计上成功演示了该方法，并进一步将其推广到多电路框架，能够跨越量子子空间的有效基。

Conclusion: 有机分子无需专属训练集：通过几何投影建立的炼金术映射，仅需一次拟合的键类型投影半径即可复用现有氢化物机器学习求解器，并兼容多电路子空间基扩展方案。

Abstract: Machine-learned circuit parameters could remove one of the main bottlenecks of variational quantum eigensolvers: the classical optimization loop, with its typical barren plateaus, local minima, and costly energy and gradient evaluations, is replaced by a direct prediction. Prior work showed that machine-learning models paired with a structured circuit design can transfer across system size and structure within hydrogenic systems. The next step: Extending them to organic molecules would seem to require re-training on use-case-specific training sets. In this work, we show that this is not necessary by establishing an alchemical approach. A geometric projection places surrogate hydrogen atoms along the bond directions of the target molecule and so maps its local bonding environment onto small hydrogenic fragments. Existing hydrogenic solvers can then be reused as they are. The only new quantity is a single projection radius per bond type, which is fitted once on a small reference molecule and reused unchanged thereafter. We demonstrate the approach on the separable-pair circuit designs that have been used in prior work and extend the approach to multi-circuit approaches that span effective bases for quantum subspaces.

</details>


### [89] [One Bit, Many Records: Information Replication in Quantum Many-Body Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11446)
*Dan-Bo Zhang,Yan-Ming Chen*
