TL;DR: 提出"动力学选择"机制：未受扰动的非热参考态保持局域可辨识，而受局域扰动的分支在同一哈密顿量下扩散弛豫，从而在量子多体系统中生成可广泛读取的复制记录；并给出熵-容量判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典信息复制指单个局域编码比特在大量互不相交区域中同时可读。作者希望找到一种普适的动力学机制来解释此类记录如何产生，并刻画信息扩散的空间多重性。

Method: 以"动力学选择"为核心：对比未扰动非热参考态与局域扰动分支在相同哈密顿量下的演化；建立熵-容量判据，分析面积律参考态与体积律扰动分支的熵关系；用嵌入二聚体链与稳定的PXP动力学进行数值验证。

Result: 熵-容量判据表明：均匀面积律参考态配合体积律扰动分支，可在固定且非零的信息阈值下保证广延数量的记录；嵌入二聚体链同时展现记录增殖，且读出可靠性随碎片尺寸增大而提高；稳定的PXP动力学将该机制推广至相干递归参考态。

Conclusion: 信息扩散可由可访问记录的空间多重性来刻画；该机制给出可通过固定局域读出测量的实验特征。

Abstract: Information replication occurs when a single locally encoded classical bit becomes simultaneously readable across extensively many disjoint regions of a quantum many-body system. Here we identify dynamical selection as a mechanism for generating these records, whereby an unperturbed nonthermal reference remains locally recognizable while its locally perturbed counterpart spreads and relaxes under the same Hamiltonian. We establish an entropy-capacity criterion showing that a uniform area-law reference paired with a volume-law perturbed branch guarantees extensive records at a fixed, nonzero information threshold. An embedded-dimer chain demonstrates both record proliferation and increasing readout reliability with fragment size, while stabilized PXP dynamics extends the mechanism to a coherently recurrent reference. These results characterize information spreading through the spatial multiplicity of accessible records and provide experimental signatures measurable via fixed local readouts.

</details>


### [90] [Quantum State Reconstruction from Sklar-Type Relational Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11468)
*Yi-Yu Lin,Jie-Chen Jin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 借助Sklar定理将量子态Born概率表中各子系统的边际统计丢弃，仅保留关系数据，并证明当这些关系数据遍历所有context收集后，量子相容性约束足以重建任意不可约多比特量子态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典概率论中Sklar定理将联合分布的边际统计与依赖结构（copula）严格分离。作者希望把这一"边际—依赖"分离视角移植到由Born规则诱导的量子依赖context的概率表上，考察量子态的哪部分信息由"关系数据"承载。

Method: 对给定量子态，逐context考察Born规则给出的经典概率表，丢弃各方的边际统计，只保留其间的关联（关系）数据；再将所有context下的关系数据汇集，利用量子力学中非平凡的相容性约束进行量子态重建，并证明重建的充分性。

Result: 证明结论：仅凭Sklar型关系数据（边际被逐表舍弃）即可重建任意不可约多比特量子态，即被丢弃的各子系统局部信息可被完全恢复。

Conclusion: 量子相容性约束使Sklar型关系数据携带了重建完整量子态（含局部边际信息）的全部必要信息，作者将这一过程称为"基于Sklar型关系数据的量子态重建"。

Abstract: Sklar's theorem in classical probability theory rigorously separates the marginal statistics of a joint probability distribution from its dependence structure data. We apply this perspective to the context-dependent classical probability tables induced by the Born rule for a quantum state. For each Born probability table of a given quantum state, we discard the marginal statistics of the individual parties, retaining only the relational data among them. Yet we prove that, when these classical relational data are collected over all contexts, the nontrivial quantum compatibility constraints inherent in quantum mechanics suffice to reconstruct any irreducible multiqubit quantum state itself. This means that the local information of the subsystems, discarded table by table, is fully recovered. We call this quantum-state reconstruction from Sklar-type relational data.

</details>


### [91] [Weight-Space Programming of Variational Quantum Circuits: Large Language Models Write Quantum Circuit Parameters Without an Optimizer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11537)
*Debarshi Kundu,Swaroop Ghosh,Vasant G Honavar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究提出将"权重空间编程"从经典网络推广到量子线路，让前沿LLM直接写出变分量子线路的角度参数，无需任何损失/梯度反馈，并系统测试其在VQE、QAOA和手写数字分类等任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分量子算法(VQA)依赖反复的量子设备评估来优化线路角度，常面临梯度消失与高昂优化成本。作者想验证：大语言模型能否凭借对问题与线路结构的理解，直接生成这些角度参数。

Method: 向前沿LLM(gpt)提供问题、线路与门约定，其输出的角度被严格按原样执行，不返回损失、梯度或准确率反馈。共139次请求，覆盖手写数字分类(3 vs 8)、自旋链与H$_2$的VQE基态估计、MaxCut QAOA；对照组包括随机权重、经典优化器、以及以LLM权重为初值的优化；另测试LLM自行设计线路的能力(30例)。

Result: 当参数具有可解释结构时，LLM匹配或接近优化器：每个VQE问题达到能谱的95–100%，闭式给出精确H$_2$基态；对每个图均找到最优深度一QAOA角度；用12–18个手写权重在3 vs 8分类上达93–96%准确率，优于同十样本训练的优化器。作为热启动，中位数1次评估即达最优。自主设计线路时，30例均选择数据依赖的旋转链(二分类用1量子比特，十分类每比特一链)，十分类准确率65%。但在不透明权重空间失效：固定线路超过30个权重时准确率降至随机水平，而优化器反而提升；对门约定敏感，42个QAOA回复中6个混淆混合角符号而失败。全部提示、回复与评分已公开。

Conclusion: LLM可在可解释的参数化空间中直接编写VQA角度并作为有效热启动，但在高维不透明权重空间及存在约定歧义时表现脆弱，提示需结合结构先验与约定校验。

Abstract: Variational quantum algorithms (VQAs) optimize circuit angles through repeated quantum-device evaluations, often facing vanishing gradients and high optimization costs. We ask whether a large language model (LLM) can write these angles directly. Extending *weight-space programming* from classical networks to quantum circuits, we provide a frontier LLM (\gpt) with the problem, circuit, and gate conventions. Its angles are executed exactly as written, without returning losses, gradients, or accuracy feedback. Across 139 requests, we test handwritten-digit classification, VQE ground-state estimation for spin chains and H$_2$, and MaxCut QAOA, comparing against random weights, classical optimization, and optimization initialized with LLM weights on the same circuit. When parameters have interpretable structure, the LLM matches or approaches the optimizer: it achieves 95--100\% of the energy spectrum on every VQE problem, derives the exact H$_2$ ground state in closed form, finds optimal depth-one QAOA angles for every graph, and reaches 93--96\% accuracy on digits 3 versus 8 with 12--18 hand-written weights, outperforming an optimizer trained on the same ten examples. Its weights are also strong warm starts: optimization reaches its best value after a median of one evaluation rather than hundreds. Allowed to design circuits, the LLM chooses data-dependent rotation chains encoding pixel importance in all 30 cases (one qubit for binary tasks, one chain per qubit for ten classes), reaching 65\% accuracy across ten digits. Yet the approach fails in opaque weight spaces: accuracy drops to chance when fixed circuits exceed 30 weights, while optimization improves. It is also sensitive to gate conventions: 6 of 42 QAOA replies reverse mixing-angle signs and fail. We release all prompts, replies, and scores.

</details>


### [92] [A Microscopic Model Underpinning Macroscopic QED in Active Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11557)
*Daigo Oue*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文为活性介质中的宏观量子电动力学建立微观基础：从受驱三能级系统导出具有非平衡稳态布居的有效二能级描述与集体朗之万动力学，使电磁极化率与量子噪声流关联来自同一微观稳态，并给出有效温度形式的涨落—耗散关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规宏观量子电动力学（QED）中，极化率与噪声流关联通常作为唯象输入，二者缺乏统一的微观来源，尤其对具有非平衡布居的活性（增益/吸收）介质，其光学响应与量子噪声的关系尚不清晰。

Method: 以受驱三能级系统为起点，绝热消去辅助能级得到具有非平衡稳态布居的有效二能级模型；采用量子中心极限（高斯近似）方法得到集体朗之万动力学，由此在同一微观稳态下同时计算电磁极化率与量子噪声流关联，并与常规宏观QED的库算符建立对应；进一步以有效库占据数和双真空模纯化解释噪声统计，导出有效温度型涨落—耗散关系。

Result: 光学响应由布居差决定，而噪声关联分别依赖于两个能级的布居；因此在光学透明点（布居差为零时），极化率虚部消失但量子涨落仍有限，二者并不同步消失，需以双真空模纯化的有效库描述；透明点的残余涨落可对邻近量子探针产生可观测的有限加热。

Conclusion: 活性介质的宏观QED可在单一微观非平衡稳态上自洽构建，光学响应与量子噪声解耦于不同布居量；透明并不等价于无噪声，残余涨落具有可观测的物理效应（探针加热），为定量描述增益/吸收介质中的量子噪声与热效应提供了框架。

Abstract: We establish a microscopic foundation for macroscopic quantum electrodynamics in active media. Starting from a driven three-level system, we derive an effective two-level description with nonequilibrium stationary populations and employ a quantum central limit approach to obtain collective Langevin dynamics. This construction yields the electromagnetic susceptibility and quantum noise-current correlations from the same microscopic stationary state, providing a direct connection to the reservoir operators of conventional macroscopic quantum electrodynamics. We show that the optical response is governed by the population difference, whereas the noise correlations depend separately on the populations of the two levels. Consequently, at optical transparency, the imaginary part of the susceptibility vanishes while quantum fluctuations remain finite. We interpret the resulting noise statistics in terms of an effective reservoir occupation and a purification involving two vacuum modes, and establish a corresponding effective-temperature fluctuation--dissipation relation. Finally, we demonstrate that the residual fluctuations at transparency can induce finite heating of a nearby quantum probe.

</details>


### [93] [A geometrical approach for obtaining exact analytical solutions for a scalar field in a one-dimensional dynamical cavity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11595)
*Maxwell F. de Araújo,Odemar Julião do N. Neto,Stanley S. Coelho,Jeferson Danilo L. Silva,Alessandra N. Braga,Danilo T. Alves*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将 Cole-Schieve 几何方法从"数值手段"重新诠释为求解 Moore 方程的解析工具，给出多个新的精确解析解，并证明可据此把已知解析解从有限几类推广到无穷集合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态 Casimir 效应的精确分析依赖 Moore 方程，但文献中其精确解析解极少，仅限少数特定运动规律；Cole 与 Schieve（1995）的几何方法虽给出初始静止腔的通用公式，却长期只被当作数值方法使用。

Method: 在 (1+1) 维、Dirichlet 边界条件下，将场用满足 Moore 泛函方程的函数表示；把 Cole-Schieve 几何构造作为解析推导框架，并加以推广——证明只要在某一时空区域中 Moore 函数已确定，就可据此构造精确解。

Result: 得到若干原创精确解析解：匀速移动镜面的膨胀腔、镜面渐近趋近静止镜面的收缩腔；并重新导出 Law（1994）关于共振镜面运动的第一个解析解；推广后的几何方法可通过对已知运动规律的组合生成新解。

Conclusion: Cole-Schieve 方法不仅是数值工具，更是强有力的解析求解手段；推广后的几何途径可将 Moore 方程的解析解从文献中有限的几类运动扩展为无穷集合。

Abstract: The dynamical Casimir effect was first predicted by G. T. Moore in 1969, who considered a real scalar field inside a cavity with a moving mirror in ($1+1$)D, satisfying Dirichlet boundary conditions. Moore wrote the general solution for the field in terms of a function that satisfies a functional equation nowadays known as the Moore equation.In the literature, exact analytical solutions to the Moore equation form a rather small set, restricted to specific classes of laws of motion. Cole and Schieve (1995) developed a geometrical approach that yielded a general formula for the solution of the Moore equation for cavities initially at rest. However, its application has always been done through numerical calculations, and their approach has therefore long been regarded solely as a numerical method. In this work, we show that their method can also be employed as a powerful method for deriving exact analytical solutions, and show the applicability of this approach to deriving analytical solutions to the Moore equation. We obtain original exact analytical solutions for an expanding cavity with a uniformly moving mirror, a contracting cavity with a mirror that approaches the static one asymptotically, and, in addition, we recover the first analytical solution to the Moore equation for a resonant mirror motion, originally obtained by Law in 1994. Moreover, we extend the Cole-Schieve approach, showing that exact solutions can be constructed from any spacetime region where the Moore function has already been determined. We show that this extended geometrical approach allows us to expand the set of known analytical solutions to the Moore equation from the few classes of motion reported in the literature to an infinite set, as it enables the treatment of new classes of laws of motion obtained by combining those presented here with each other as well as with previously known classes of trajectories.

</details>


### [94] [Proof of the Aaronson-Ambainis Conjecture: An Exposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11626)
*Guy Blanc,Jordan Docter,Carmen Strassle,Li-Yang Tan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文是对Aaronson-Ambainis猜想一个新证明的阐释性综述，该猜想称每个从{0,1}^n到[0,1]的d次多项式必存在影响度至少为poly(Var(p)/d)的变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Aaronson-Ambainis猜想是量子查询复杂度理论中的核心猜想：若成立，则判定问题的量子查询算法在大多数输入上可被经典高效模拟，并给出随机预言机世界与无相对化世界中BPP vs. BQP问题的近乎等价。作者希望以教学性、易读的方式呈现这一近期新证明。

Method: 将证明组织为四个主要步骤，围绕布尔函数分析与概率组合学的已有文献展开；为突出核心思想而刻意不优化参数，因此所得指数弱于原始证明。证明由David Turturean借助PSI研发的研究系统与一个非公开Anthropic模型发现，作者随后加以理解与整理。

Result: 给出该猜想的完整阐释性证明框架，并说明各步骤与布尔函数分析、概率组合学文献的联系；所获参数与指数弱于原始证明，但逻辑结构完整。

Conclusion: 该阐释降低了新证明的理解门槛，凸显其关键技术思想；但本文非为会议或期刊发表而作，且参数未优化，故不提供最优界。

Abstract: We give an exposition of a recent proof of the Aaronson-Ambainis conjecture, which states that every degree-$d$ polynomial $p : \{0,1\}^n \to [0,1]$ has a variable of influence at least $\mathrm{poly}(\mathrm{Var}(p)/d)$. This implies that quantum query algorithms for decision problems can be efficiently simulated classically on most inputs. It also yields a near-equivalence between the status of the $\mathsf{BPP}$ vs. $\mathsf{BQP}$ problem in the random oracle and unrelativized worlds.
  We organize the proof around four main steps and discuss how each connects to the literature on the analysis of boolean functions and probabilistic combinatorics. To bring out the key ideas, we do not optimize parameters, and our presentation yields weaker exponents than the original.
  $\textbf{Authors' note.}$ The proof was discovered by David Turturean using a research system developed at Physical Superintelligence (PSI), in combination with a non-public Anthropic model. The proof was then shared with the authors, who worked to understand it and prepare this exposition. Anthropic's public models were used in this process.
  The authors did not receive financial compensation from Anthropic or PSI. This article is not intended for publication at a conference or in a journal.

</details>


### [95] [Pauli propagation in adaptive local frames](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11647)
*Jonnathan Pineda-Jiménez,Mario Collura*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: Pauli传播的成本取决于算符展开所用的局域基；作者将Pauli复杂度分解为局域旋转不变的占据部分与可由合适基消除的"flavour"部分，并据此提出帧自适应Pauli传播(FAPP)：逐门将每个量子比特的基旋转到算符局域极化的主轴，从而显著降低截断误差与内存开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Pauli传播的代价依赖于演化算符在何种局域基下展开，但常规做法使用固定基，可能引入大量本可消除的Pauli串。作者希望理解并利用"基选择"这一自由度，以在不改变算法内存结构的前提下降低复杂度。

Method: 1) 理论分解：将Pauli复杂度拆为占据部分（局域旋转下不变，受算符质量与长度分布下界约束）与flavour部分（可由合适基消除）；2) 用局域极化方向预测任意局域基可获得的增益；3) 提出FAPP算法，逐门把每个量子比特的基旋转至算符局域极化的主轴，保持精确性且完全兼容Pauli传播的内存结构。

Result: 在横纵场Ising链上，固定内存下截断误差最多降低一个数量级，内存降低约三倍且与体系尺寸无关；在无限二维格点上，达到给定认证精度所需Pauli串最多减少7倍；在掺杂T门的Clifford电路中减少2至8倍。该简单规则可达到最优局域帧的大部分效果。

Conclusion: Pauli传播的复杂度可分占据与flavour两部分，后者可被局域基选择消除；基于局域极化主轴的FAPP是一种简单、通用且无额外结构开销的基选择方案，能显著提升截断误差与内存效率，并可用于预测局域帧的理论增益上限。

Abstract: The cost of Pauli propagation depends on the local basis in which the evolving operator is expanded. We show that its Pauli complexity splits into an occupation part, invariant under local rotations and bounded from below by the operator mass and length distributions, and a flavour part that a suitable basis can remove. We introduce frame-adaptive Pauli propagation (FAPP), which rotates the basis of every qubit, gate by gate, to the principal axes of the operator's local polarisation, exactly and within the memory structure of Pauli propagation. This simple rule achieves most of what the best local frame can, and the polarisation predicts how much any local frame can gain. In the Ising chain in transverse and longitudinal fields, FAPP lowers the truncation error at fixed memory by up to an order of magnitude and the memory by a size-independent factor of three; on the infinite two-dimensional lattice it needs up to seven times fewer strings for a given certified accuracy, and in Clifford circuits doped with T gates two to eight times fewer.

</details>


### [96] [Model-family selection and recovery-gate design in generator-based noise tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11748)
*Long Huang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 将生成器模型噪声层析成像中的"家族选择"重新表述为闭式散粒噪声底限假设检验，底限由绝对偏差而非相对残差决定，并据此对设计进行定价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上家族选择被视为硬件属性，缺乏在实验前可计算的定量判据，且设计与家族不可分离评价，需要统一框架。

Method: 推导闭式散粒底限 N*=λ_req/(r_b S)²；用同一映射同时读取固定残差的指纹与排序设计的Fisher信息；在64种设计下重建指纹并比较底限迁移因子。

Result: 两平台在design-blind块分别为98.1%与48.0%；参考点底限为1.1×10⁷与6.7×10⁶ shots/component，故10⁵ shots时检验功效仅0.053、水平0.05，报告残差为噪声；设计重建使底限迁移68与1.7倍，最精确设计比最便宜测试贵10倍；三门时rms随布局变化2.7与3.9倍；五量子比特底限迁移21倍、判决成本10⁹ shots。

Conclusion: 设计问题本质是条件数问题而非秩问题；底限首先是对系统误差控制的要求，其次才是散粒数计数。

Abstract: Family selection in generator-model noise tomography, usually quoted as a property of the hardware, is a hypothesis test whose shot floor is closed form, $N^{*}=λ_{\mathrm{req}}/(r_{\mathrm{b}} S)^{2}$, controlled by the absolute bias $r_{\mathrm{b}} S$ rather than by the relative residual $r_{\mathrm{b}}$, and therefore computable for any device before a single shot is taken. The same computation prices the recovery gates, because the fingerprint that fixes a family's residual and the Fisher information that ranks the designs are read off the same map: there is no design-independent verdict and no family-independent design optimum. Whether a design can move that price is structural, not numerical: a bias confined to the block every design measures identically is design-blind, while one that reaches the code blocks can be repriced by a factor of sixty-eight. The two platforms here are its extremes, at $98.1\%$ and $48.0\%$ in the design-blind block. At the reference point the floor is $1.1\times10^{7}$ and $6.7\times10^{6}$ shots per component for the two families, so at the $10^{5}$ shots a run spends the test has power $0.053$ at a level of $0.05$ and the residual it reports is shot noise. Rebuilding the fingerprint under each of the sixty-four designs moves the floor by $68$ on one platform and $1.7$ on the other, and the most precise design costs ten times more shots for the verdict than the cheapest to test. All sixty-four placements are full rank, so the design problem is one of conditioning: at three gates the rms varies by factors of $2.7$ and $3.9$ with placement alone, while the optimum over counts is flat. At five qubits the same rule moves the floor by $21$, while the verdict itself costs $10^{9}$ shots; checked against the calibration of a real three-qubit register, the floor is a requirement on systematic control before it is a shot count.

</details>


### [97] [Advantage Distillation in Paired-State Quantum Key Distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11754)
*Luis Adrián Lizama-Pérez,José Mauricio López-Romero*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究配对态回环(Loop-Back)QKD在固定光学站与轻量便携终端架构下的优势蒸馏，提出基于奇偶校验的最小连通图三方块筛选方法，给出精确的接收概率、残余误差律、保留率与模块化安全定理，并在单光子无源分光信道下获得正的熵界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Loop-Back QKD 中一方为轻量便携终端、另一方为固定光学站，实际信道与探测条件使双方原始比特记录易出现不一致；需要在传统密钥协商(reconciliation)之前引入低成本、可量化安全的预处理，以降低残余错误并抑制窃听者的猜测优势。

Method: 协议层面：Alice 制备两种偏振编码脉冲，Bob 对每个脉冲选择并施加偏振滤波，Alice 由返回的探测图样推断其选择。优势蒸馏步骤在两个独立公开奇偶校验下、在最小连通图上选取一致的"三条两比特记录"块；证明奇偶多边形的闭合边冗余，同一顶点集任意生成树具有相同接受集。理论上推导精确接受概率、残余误差律与保留率，并建立模块化安全定理，界定在泄露校验子(syndrome)与块接受后对手可获得的额外猜测优势（含任意量子边信息）；同时对特定单光子无源分光窃听信道进行定量评估。

Result: 在独立比特错误模型下，三方块滤波将首阶误差项由 e 降至 e^2；明确核算了被丢弃记录与公开信息的代价；对设定的单光子无源分光信道得到正的剩余熵（条件熵）界，并给出接受块的精确安全量。

Conclusion: 该奇偶校验型优势蒸馏通过后选择检测不一致，而非纠正单个比特错误，定位为常规协商之前的预处理环节，具有可证明的接受集结构与安全界；架构上指向两个延伸方向：混合式滤波与协商的结合，以及多个便携用户共享同一量子站的密钥建立。

Abstract: We investigate advantage distillation for paired-state Loop-Back quantum key distribution, with a fixed optical station serving a lightweight portable terminal. Alice prepares two polarization-encoded pulses, Bob selects and applies a polarization filter to each pulse, and Alice infers his selections from the returned detection pattern. The proposed advantage-distillation step selects consistent blocks of three two-bit records using two independent public parity checks on a minimal connected graph. The closing edge of a parity polygon is shown to be redundant: any spanning tree of the same vertices has the same acceptance set. We derive exact acceptance probabilities, residual-error laws, and retention rates. Under independent bit errors, the three-record filter reduces the leading error term from e to e^2, while its cost in discarded records and public information is accounted for explicitly. A modular security theorem bounds the additional guessing advantage available to an adversary after syndrome disclosure and block acceptance, including arbitrary quantum side information. We also evaluate a specified single-photon passive-tap optical channel, obtaining a positive conditional entropy bound and exact accepted-block security quantities. This parity-based advantage-distillation step detects discrepancies by postselection; it does not correct individual errors and is intended to precede conventional reconciliation. The architecture suggests two further directions: hybrid filtering and reconciliation, and key establishment between portable users accessing a shared quantum station.

</details>


### [98] [An optimal quantum strategy for the $2$-hat game](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11759)
*Georgios Baziotis,Sang-Jun Park,Sang-Gyun Youn*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文将双人帽子游戏视为2输入3输出的非局域博弈，计算其量子交换值与量子值相等，均为(15+√33)/36≈0.576238，并证明该优势由共享非最大纠缠三能级态实现，同时得到无信号值2/3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定帽子游戏H_2在量子策略下能否超越经典策略，并厘清量子张量积值与量子交换值是否一致，是该非局域博弈的核心量化问题。

Method: 把双人帽子游戏建模为2输入、3输出的非局域博弈；以共享非最大纠缠qutrit态构造达到最优量子优势的策略；利用博弈在二面体群D_8下的对称性及其表示论约化矩阵问题的规模，从而证明最优性。

Result: 量子值与量子交换值相等：ω_q(H_2)=ω_qc(H_2)=(15+√33)/36≈0.576238；最优量子优势由非最大纠缠三能级态实现；无信号值ω_ns(H_2)=2/3。

Conclusion: 帽子游戏H_2的量子值与交换量子值重合，最优量子优势可由非最大纠缠qutrit态达成，且严格低于无信号上界2/3，表明该博弈的量子—无信号间隙存在。

Abstract: We study the two-player hat game as a two-input, three-output non-local game. We calculate the quantum commuting value and show that it coincides with the quantum one. In particular, we show that \[ω_{\rm q}(H_2)=ω_{\mathrm{qc}}(H_2)=\frac{15+\sqrt{33}}{36}\approx 0.576238.\] We achieve the optimal quantum advantage using a shared non-maximally entangled qutrit state. To establish optimality, we exploit the game's symmetry under the dihedral group $D_8$ and apply its representation theory to substantially reduce the complexity of the underlying matrix problem. We also determine the no-signalling value $ω_{\mathrm{ns}}(H_2)=2/3$.

</details>


### [99] [Super-resolution quantum imaging via multiphoton correlation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11768)
*Yu Gu,Yiqi Song,Meixue Chen,Yuchen He,Hui Chen,Huaibin Zheng,Yu Zhou,Fuli Li,Zhuo Xu,Jianbin Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 利用经典热光，通过Hanbury Brown-Twiss干涉仪双臂光子数分辨的多光子关联，实现超越衍射极限的超分辨成像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统成像分辨率受衍射极限根本性约束，作者希望在不使用非经典光源的前提下突破该极限。

Method: 基于经典热光，在HBT干涉仪两臂中分辨探测的光子数，测量光子数关联函数；其关联峰或谷的宽度可通过平均光子数调节。实验测量时间与空间超分辨增强，并以双针孔为原理验证对象，通过双光子与零光子关联成像。

Result: 实验获得时间超分辨增强最高11.8倍、空间超分辨增强最高5.1倍；标准鬼成像完全无法分辨的双针孔经由双光子-零光子关联变得清晰可辨。

Conclusion: 该超分辨成像原理可理解为经典超分辨成像与量子超分辨成像的结合，为超分辨成像与光刻提供通用平台，并为探索经典与非经典现象边界开辟新途径。

Abstract: The resolution of conventional imaging is fundamentally constrained by the diffraction limit. Here we demonstrate that this limit can be overcome using classical thermal light, through the correlation of multiphoton subsystems. By resolving the photon numbers detected in the two arms of a Hanbury Brown-Twiss interferometer, the correlation function exhibits a narrowed peak or dip whose width is tunable via the average photon number. Temporal and spatial super-resolution enhancements up to 11.8-fold and 5.1-fold, respectively, are experimentally achieved. As a proof-of-principle experiment, a double-pinhole that is completely unresolved by standard ghost imaging becomes clearly distinguishable via the correlation of two-photon versus zero-photon. The principle of this super-resolution imaging can be interpreted as a combination of classical super-resolution imaging and quantum super-resolution imaging. This work provides a versatile platform for super-resolution imaging and lithography, and opens a new avenue for exploring the boundary between classical and nonclassical phenomena.

</details>


### [100] [Long-distance quantum secure direct communication with Gottesman-Kitaev-Preskill qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11822)
*Meng-Dong Zhu,Jia-Wei Ying,Qi Zhang,Shi-Pu Gu,Xing-Fu Wang,Lan Zhou,Yu-Bo Sheng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出基于GKP编码的量子安全直接通信（QSDC）协议，利用第三代量子中继器克服光子指数衰减，并建立成本优化框架刻画通信距离、保密容量与中继资源的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GKP码具有格周期性结构，可有效纠正相空间中噪声引起的小位移误差，已被用于构建量子中继器长距离纠缠信道及QKD分发随机密钥；作者希望借助GKP编码实现既能直接传输秘密消息、又能保护通信人员安全的QSDC，并解决光子指数衰减损耗问题。

Method: 采用GKP编码设计QSDC协议，引入第三代量子中继器补偿光子指数衰减损耗；通过数值仿真评估安全传输距离与压缩度关系，并构建基于成本的优化框架，刻画安全通信距离、保密容量与中继资源之间的权衡关系。

Result: 数值仿真表明，在23 dB压缩度下可实现超过1000 km的安全传输距离；成本优化框架给出了中继部署优化方案，为可扩展量子中继架构提供系统化设计策略。

Conclusion: 结果证明了将GKP纠错与长距离QSDC及量子网络相结合具有潜力。

Abstract: The Gottesman-Kitaev-Preskill (GKP) code possesses a lattice periodic structure that enables the effective correction of small displacement errors induced by noise in phase space, which is indispensable for quantum computation. The GKP code was also used to construct a long-distance entanglement channel in quantum repeaters and distribute random keys in quantum key distribution (QKD). Using GKP encoding, we propose a quantum secure direct communication (QSDC) protocol that not only transmits the secret message directly, but also helps protect the safety of communication personnel. The exponential attenuation loss of photons can be overcome by the third-generation quantum repeater. Numerical simulation results demonstrate that secure transmission distance exceeding 1000 km is achievable with a 23 dB squeezing level. Furthermore, we establish a cost-based optimization framework to characterize the trade-off among secure communication distance, secrecy capacity, and repeater resources, enabling optimized repeater deployment and providing a systematic design strategy for scalable quantum repeater architectures. The results demonstrate the potential of combining GKP error correction for long-distance QSDC and quantum networks.

</details>


### [101] [Rotational Encoding of Objective Functions for Quantum Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11858)
*Sonia Yang,Ali Al-Bayaty,Shanyan Chen,Marek Perkowski*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出角旋转计数器（ARC），用单量子比特寄存器上的角度旋转累加编码可分解为布尔函数加权和的目标函数，并用于有限自动机状态分配问题的量子神谕构造，在26个基准上显著降低量子电路成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往将加权布尔函数和目标函数编码为量子神谕时多采用多量子比特寄存器，资源开销大；作者希望寻找一种更紧凑的编码方式以降低量子搜索类优化中的电路成本。

Method: ARC将目标值编码为累加到单个量子比特上的旋转角度之和，而非多量子比特寄存器；理论上证明这些旋转之和等于所有可行解的目标值，并推导测量概率与目标值之间的关系。随后将该设计应用于以加权汉明距离为代价函数的有限自动机/状态机状态分配问题（二次分配问题的特例）的量子神谕构造。

Result: 在26个基准上，与基于布尔的神谕相比，采用ARC的神谕平均减少量子比特数20.8%、门数89.3%、电路深度92.4%、量子成本90.6%（四项成本指标）。

Conclusion: ARC在所评估基准上大幅降低电路成本，表明旋转编码是量子搜索算法优化中表示目标函数的一种有前景的方法。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce the Angular Rotational Counter (ARC), a novel approach to represent objective functions that can be decomposed as a weighted sum of Boolean functions. ARC encodes the objective as an accumulated sum of quantum rotations onto a single-qubit register instead of a multi-qubit register used in much of the previous literature. We mathematically establish and experimentally validate that these rotations sum to the objective value for all possible solutions and derive the relationship between the resulting measurement probabilities and the objective. This design was applied to construct a quantum oracle for the state assignment problem of finite automata and state machines using a weighted Hamming distance cost function, which is a special case of the quadratic assignment problem. We evaluate this oracle design on 26 benchmarks and compare it with corresponding Boolean-based oracles on four cost metrics. Across these benchmarks, the oracle with ARC produces a mean reduction in the qubit count of 20.8%, gate count of 89.3%, circuit depth of 92.4%, and quantum cost of 90.6%. These results demonstrate substantial reductions in circuit cost for the evaluated benchmarks and establish rotational encoding as a promising approach for representing objective functions in optimization based on quantum search algorithms.

</details>


### [102] [AI-Driven Discovery of Entanglement Witness in Spin Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11935)
*Jiwei Tian,Mouyang Cheng,Mingda Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出EntNet框架，仅用低阶可测量自旋关联量即可高精度判别多体混合态的纠缠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多体纠缠是量子科学的核心资源，但仅凭实验可及的观测量来检测纠缠仍然困难。

Method: 构造至四体阶的Pauli关联量的局域幺正不变表示，稀疏筛选16个不变特征通道，并用带旋转位置编码的Transformer学习其空间结构。

Result: 在已认证纠缠态上召回率>98%、在可分离态上误报率近零；跨系统尺寸与不同哈密顿量族（含阻挫与无序模型）迁移时精度损失极小。

Conclusion: EntNet提供了从局域关联函数数据驱动发现纠缠目击者的可迁移且物理可解释的框架。

Abstract: Entanglement is a crucial resource in quantum science and engineering, but detecting it in many-body states from experimentally accessible observables remains challenging. Here we introduce EntNet, an AI-driven framework to discover compact combinations of spin observables that accurately distinguish entangled from fully separable many-body mixed states in one-dimensional spin-$1/2$ chains governed by short-range Hamiltonians. We construct local-unitary-invariant representations of measurable Pauli correlators up to four-body order, sparsely select 16 candidate invariant feature channels, and learn their spatial structure using a Transformer architecture with rotary positional embeddings. Using only expectation values of these low-order observables, EntNet achieves $>98\%$ recall on certified entangled states with near-zero false-positive rates on separable states. Remarkably, it exhibits transferability across system sizes and distinct Hamiltonian families, including frustrated and disordered spin models, with minimal loss of accuracy. Beyond detection, EntNet provides physical insight into entanglement detection by revealing a selective spatial organization of pair correlations, while its finite-temperature decision boundary tracks the excitation gap of Jordan-Wigner quasiparticles. These results establish a transferable and physically interpretable framework for data-driven discovery of entanglement witnesses from local correlation functions.

</details>


### [103] [Parameterized equivalence checking of quantum circuit generators via weighted automata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12049)
*Pavel Chuklanov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种将"梯形程序"生成的量子电路矩阵刻画为一致矩阵乘积算子（uniform MPS/MPO），从而把六种等价性问题归约为差分自动机的零性判定，并对所有参数值给出精确或带误差界的判定结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子电路生成器（输入量子比特数与比特串）的正确性需对所有参数值验证，而固定规模（固定量子比特数）的测试无法覆盖任意 n，可能漏检错误；因此需要一种对参数族整体进行等价比对的、可判定的形式化方法。

Method: 1) 将生成器建模为 ladder program；2) 证明存在可计算阈值，超过该阈值后电路矩阵构成一致矩阵乘积算子（UMPO），其对应加权自动机维数与量子比特数无关；3) 在六种等价关系下，把等价性问题归约为差分自动机的零性（zeroness）问题；4) 借助模素数检验判定零性，输出反例或"相等"结论，并可为数值情形计算误差界。

Result: 实验中，该方法检出了固定规模检查所遗漏的错误，且未产生错误的判定结论（无误报）。

Conclusion: 利用一致矩阵乘积算子的有限维自动机表示，可对量子电路生成器实现跨全部参数值的、精确或带误差界的等价性验证，具备可判定性与实验可靠性。

Abstract: A generator builds a quantum circuit from the number of qubits and a bit string. We check a generator against a reference for all parameter values under six equivalence relations. For ladder programs, we prove that above a computable threshold the circuit matrices form a uniform matrix product operator, a weighted automaton of dimension independent of the number of qubits. Equivalence reduces to the zeroness of a difference automaton, checked modulo primes. The answer is a counterexample or "equal", exact or with a computed error bound. Experimentally, the method found errors missed by fixed-size checks and produced no incorrect verdicts.

</details>


### [104] [A Universal Fault-Tolerant Quantum Computing Architecture with Globally Driven Atom Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12098)
*Zhihao Zhang,Shuaifan Cao,Xinyang Yu,Yanyan Chen,Yueyang Min,Yingzhou Li,Zhongcheng Yu,Lei Feng,Xiaopeng Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于全局驱动双物种原子阵列的通用容错架构，以种间里德堡相互作用实现无需局部门寻址的量子比特可寻址性，构建GRAIL原生指令集并将表面码纠错与魔术态培养编译其中，得到1.43%纠错阈值与低于10^-9的魔术态逻辑错误率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可扩展量子计算需兼顾可编程性与简单、高保真控制。局部门寻址在大规模系统中带来繁重的校准负担与串扰问题，因此作者希望以原子介导的选择性替代局部寻址。

Method: 采用双物种中性原子阵列，利用种间里德堡相互作用实现比特选择性；构建通用原生指令集GRAIL（全局里德堡原子指令语言）；将表面码纠错与魔术态培养编译为GRAIL程序，并通过并行化编译抑制串扰；在硬件推断的电路级噪声模型下评估性能。

Result: 串扰误差可抑制至10^-4量级；量子存储的纠错阈值达1.43%；在实验可及的里德堡拉比频率下，培养的魔术态逻辑错误率低于10^-9。

Conclusion: 以原子介导的选择性取代局部门寻址，可消除相关校准需求，为大规模容错中性原子计算提供更简单控制的可行路径。

Abstract: Scalable quantum computation requires reconciling programmability with simple, high-fidelity control. Here, we propose a universal fault-tolerant architecture using globally driven dual-species atom arrays. In a hybrid neutral-atom system, interspecies Rydberg interactions enable qubit addressability without locally addressed quantum gates. We construct a universal native instruction set, Global Rydberg Atom Instruction Language (GRAIL), and show that crosstalk errors can be suppressed to the $10^{-4}$ level. We compile surface-code error correction and magic state cultivation into GRAIL, and parallelize the compilation to suppress crosstalk. Under a hardware-inferred circuit-level noise model, we obtain an error-correction threshold of $1.43\%$ for the resulting quantum memory and a logical error rate below $10^{-9}$ for magic states cultivated at an experimentally accessible Rydberg Rabi frequency. By replacing local gate addressing with atom-mediated selectivity, this architecture removes the associated calibration requirements, offering a route to simpler control for large-scale fault-tolerant neutral-atom computation.

</details>


### [105] [Modular and Looping Quantum Photonic Processing Unit for Quantum Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12126)
*Denis Stanev,Nicolò Spagnolo,Fabio Sciarrino*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于环路结构的模块化通用光量子计算架构，证明其具备通用性、确定性、量子线路模型兼容性、可重编程与自适应测量能力，并通过数值模拟与机器学习方法验证其基本构建模块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多种通用光量子计算架构各有优劣，亟需一种兼具高灵活性、可扩展性且物理元件数量更少的架构，以降低实验实现难度。

Method: 提出一种环路（loop-based）模块化架构，理论证明其通用性、确定性与量子线路模型兼容性；并利用机器学习技术对一组基本线性与非线性光学元件进行数值模拟，以获得架构所需的构建模块。

Result: 理论证明该架构是通用的、确定性的、与量子线路模型兼容的，且可重编程并支持自适应测量；数值模拟展示了其在具体实例中的运行效果，并揭示了架构的优缺点。

Conclusion: 该环路模块化架构显著减少了实验实现所需的物理元件数量，具备良好的可扩展性，为未来物理实现指明发展方向。

Abstract: Photonic processors are emerging as promising platforms in the race towards the realization of practical universal quantum computers. Several architectures with universal capabilities have been proposed for their implementation via a photonic platform, each with their own set of benefits and drawbacks. In this work we propose an architecture for a universal photonic quantum computer. The proposed architecture is modular, allowing for a high degree of flexibility and scalability, and relies on a loop-based structure, significantly reducing the number of physical components required to implement it experimentally. We prove that our architecture is universal, deterministic, and compatible with the quantum circuital model, as well as reprogrammable and capable of adaptive measurements. Furthermore, we showcase its functioning via numerical simulation on specific examples, by using machine learning techniques on a set of fundamental linear and nonlinear optical components to obtain some of the building blocks used in the architecture. The obtained results can be used to highlight the benefits and the drawbacks of the proposed architecture, as well as the route for future developments towards its physical implementation.

</details>


### [106] [High Rate Quantum Block Turbo Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12190)
*Khaled Jebari,Luiz Anet Neto,Ramesh Pyndiah,Jean-Louis de Bougrenet de la Tocnaye*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究采用不同分量码构造的新型高速率量子分组Turbo码，并考察码参数变化及分量码顺序对Turbo译码性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子分组Turbo码是乘积形式级联量子纠错码的迭代译码技术，需要探索高速率设计并厘清参数与结构对性能的作用。

Method: 以乘积码形式级联不同分量码构造高速率量子分组Turbo码，进行迭代译码，并系统改变分量码参数与分量码排列顺序进行对比分析。

Result: 获得具有不同分量码组合的新型高速率量子分组Turbo码，并明确了码参数变化及分量码顺序对Turbo译码性能的影响规律。

Conclusion: 分量码参数与顺序是影响量子分组Turbo码性能的重要设计因素，可为高速率量子纠错码的构造提供依据。

Abstract: Quantum Block Turbo Code is an iterative decoding technique for concatenated quantum error correction codes in product form. In this paper, we study new high rate quantum block turbo codes with different constituent codes. We examine the impact of the change of codes parameters and the order of constituent codes on turbo performance.

</details>


### [107] [Testing non-classical gravitational phase dynamics under strong dephasing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12229)
*Tianfeng Feng,Vlatko Vedral*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究在退相位版Bose–Marletto–Vedral装置中，即使纠缠被环境退相干淹没，仍可从动力学中区分引力诱导的条件相位演化与经典中介者诱导的被动随机局域相位模型，从而给出强退相位区间的操作性检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: “引力是否必须是量子的”常用两质量能否通过引力相互作用产生纠缠来判定，但弱引力相互作用与环境退相干竞争，最终态可能无纠缠而引力的相位相干性依然存在，因此需要考察退相位情形下动力学还能提供什么信息。

Method: 在充分分离路径下，牛顿效应主要表现为累积相位而非干涉仪两臂间的布居转移（由波包重叠与偏转估计量化，并与物质波引力相位实验相符）。在独立标定的局域which-path退相位固定条件下，证明引力条件相位演化与所有由经典中介者诱导的被动随机局域相位模型之间存在精确的有限时间区分，并借助对角PPT松弛将比较扩展到保布居的经典测量与反馈。

Result: 即使所选输入在任意时刻保持可分离，亦可在有限时间内严格区分引力条件相位演化与被动随机局域相位模型；该区分在退相位存在时依然成立，只是灵敏度有所改变；经对角PPT松弛后可推广至更一般的经典测量与反馈模型。

Conclusion: 结果为强退相位区间提供了一种检验引力相位动力学的操作性方案，但其有效性以明确且物理上合理的经典中介者模型为前提假设。

Abstract: Whether gravity must be quantum is often approached by asking whether two masses can become entangled through their mutual gravitational interaction. In practice, the weak interaction competes with environmental decoherence, so the final state may contain no entanglement even when the underlying gravitational phase remains coherent. We investigate what can still be learned from the dynamics in a dephased Bose--Marletto--Vedral setup. The dominant Newtonian effect for well-separated paths is an accumulated phase rather than transfer between interferometer arms, a regime motivated by matter-wave gravitational phase experiments and quantified here by wavepacket-overlap and deflection estimates. Keeping independently characterized local which-path dephasing fixed, we prove an exact finite-time distinction between the gravitational conditional-phase evolution and every passive stochastic local-phase model (induced by a classical mediator), including regimes where the chosen input remains separable at all times. The distinction survives, with a modified sensitivity, when the comparison is enlarged to population-preserving classical measurement and feedback through a diagonal PPT relaxation. These results identify an operational test of gravitational phase dynamics in the strong-dephasing regimes, conditional on explicit and physically motivated classical mediator models.

</details>


### [108] [Commuting-check gauging for fault-tolerant logical measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12236)
*Zongyuan Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 引入"补全量子比特"(completion qubits)使规范(gauge)后的校验在Clifford Z₂ 对称下全局对易,保持码空间、距离与LDPC性质,并据此实现容错逻辑测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gauging 把逻辑算符变成局部校验的乘积,可用于逻辑测量与格点手术;但这些校验在码空间外可能不对易,测量其中一个会随机化另一个的结果,导致综合症不稳定与容错分析困难。

Method: 对量子稳定子码的 Clifford Z₂ 对称性,为每对非对易校验增加一个补全量子比特使其对易;推广到 qudit 并分析素数维下补全校验的 Clifford 层级;利用全局对易性界定无故障轮次间综合症的稳定性与故障传播;通过规范化与反规范化距离 d 的母码实现逻辑测量。

Result: 补全保持规范码空间与距离、校验仍为 Clifford,并保持 LDPC 性质(常数因子量子比特开销);可得与测量轮数无关的故障传播界;qubit 码下规范-反规范实现电路故障距离 Ω(d)、O(d) 轮的逻辑测量;实例中将两个 toric 码规范化为 D(D₄) 量子双码并返回,显式电路的电路故障距离等于母码距离。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了基于对易校验的容错逻辑测量框架,可应用于格点手术制备魔法态,并为规范码的容错测量与综合症提取奠定理论基础。

Abstract: Gauging turns a logical operator into a product of local checks, providing a route to logical measurement and lattice surgery. These checks can fail to commute outside the code space, so measuring one can randomize the outcome of another. In this work, we introduce completion qubits that make the checks commute for Clifford $\mathbb{Z}_2$ symmetries of qubit stabilizer codes. Adding one completion qubit per noncommuting pair preserves the gauged code space and its distance while keeping the checks Clifford. Completion also preserves the low-density parity-check property with a constant-factor qubit overhead. We generalize completion to qudits and establish bounds on the Clifford-hierarchy level of the completed checks in prime dimensions. Global commutation ensures that a fault-free round fixes the syndrome until the next fault and allows us to bound fault propagation independently of the number of measurement rounds. For qubit codes, we show that gauging and ungauging a distance-$d$ parent code implements a logical measurement with circuit fault distance $Ω(d)$ in $O(d)$ rounds. We demonstrate the construction by gauging two toric codes into the $D(D_4)$ quantum double and back, with an explicit circuit whose circuit fault distance equals the parent code distance. Our results provide a framework for fault-tolerant logical measurements using commuting checks, with applications to magic-state preparation by lattice surgery.

</details>


### [109] [Deploying QKD on the fiber links of the National Quantum Communication Infrastructure in Sweden](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12279)
*Giulio Foletto,Mira Abu Yahia,Shreya Gouravaram Navalur,Carlos Brotons Montoro,Hilma Karlsson,Dena Karunianto Wibowo,Pascal Lefebvre,Katia Gallo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文报告瑞典国家量子通信基础设施部署中光纤链路的表征流程，用简化模型将可测链路参数与量子密钥分发密钥率关联，并通过一个月的实测确认可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧洲正部署QKD以评估其在实际场景中的性能与实用性；部署前需勘测量子信道光纤链路特性，并与QKD设备参数交叉核对，以确保兼容性与最优性能。

Method: 提出链路表征的测量流程与QKD适用性评估方法；构建面向QKD性能本质的简化模型，将可测物理量映射到影响成码率的关键因素；并在实际链路上开展为期一个月的测试。

Result: 建立了可测参数与成码率之间的对应关系，并在其中一条链路上通过为期一个月的成功测试验证了QKD的可行性。

Conclusion: 所提链路表征与评估流程可用于QKD部署前的兼容性判断，实测结果支持在该基础设施链路上开展QKD。

Abstract: Quantum key distribution (QKD) is being deployed across Europe to evaluate its performance and practicality in real-world scenarios. When preparing this deployment, it is important to survey the properties of the fiber links that will be used for the quantum channel, cross-checking them with those of QKD devices, to ensure compatibility and optimal performance. We report here our studies from the Swedish chapter of this endeavor, the National Quantum Communication Infrastructure in Sweden. We focus on procedures to characterize the links and evaluate their suitability for QKD. We relate measurable properties to the key factors that affect the secret key rate using a simplified model that aims at the essence of QKD performance. Finally, we confirm the feasibility of QKD on one of our links by reporting a one-month long successful test.

</details>


### [110] [Calibration-free quantum thermometry with strain-swept two-level defects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12280)
*Asghar ullah,Özgür E. Müstecaplıoğlu,Mehmet Cengiz Onbaşlı*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 约瑟夫森结势垒中的本征二能级缺陷可作局部温度计：利用其布居响应形状，无需标定读出偏移与对比度即可测温；但边际化未知参数带来可辨识性约束，窄扫描下可用信息按扫描宽度的四次方缩放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导量子比特工作在特定温区，局部温度监测很重要；而传统测温需预先标定读出偏移与对比度，缺乏免标定的原初温度传感方案。

Method: 以约瑟夫森结势垒中本征二能级缺陷的布居响应形状为测温信号，对读出偏移与对比度进行边际化，推导可辨识性要求与信息标度律；并以应变扫描（沿双曲线一支改变非对称性）的缺陷实例进行拟合精度投影分析。

Result: 对Δ0/h=1.3 GHz的已报道缺陷，沿双曲线一支扫描至2.69 GHz（非对称性偏移2.36 GHz，即8 V压电电压），单缺陷拟合在20 mK下投影精度为0.58%；同一扫描还可过定约束玻尔兹曼形式并识别显著非热偏离。

Conclusion: 本征结缺陷可作为免标定局部测温资源，并将可辨识性确立为原初温度传感的关键设计准则。

Abstract: Superconducting qubits operate within a well-defined temperature range, making local temperature monitoring important. Here we show that intrinsic two-level defects in Josephson-junction barriers can act as local thermometers by exploiting the shape of their population response, without requiring calibration of the readout offset or contrast. Marginalizing over these unknown parameters imposes an identifiability requirement absent from single-parameter thermometric bounds: for narrow sweeps, usable information scales as the fourth power of the sweep width. Hence, a narrow sweep becomes locally indistinguishable from a change in offset or contrast. We illustrate this principle with a strain-swept two-level defect in a Josephson junction. For a reported defect with tunneling energy $Δ_{0}/h=\SI{1.3}{\giga\hertz}$, swept along one branch of its hyperbola to \SI{2.69}{\giga\hertz} (an asymmetry excursion of \SI{2.36}{\giga\hertz}, or \SI{8}{\volt} of piezo voltage), the analysis projects a single-defect fit precision of \SI{0.58}{\percent} at \SI{20}{\milli\kelvin}. The resulting response enables temperature estimation without prior calibration of the readout offset and contrast, while the same sweep overdetermines the Boltzmann form and identifies significant deviations from thermality. These results establish intrinsic junction defects as a resource for calibration-free local thermometry and highlight identifiability as a key design criterion for primary temperature sensing.

</details>


### [111] [Attainable and unattainable quantum precision limits in ultrastrong light--matter coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12471)
*Wangjun Lu,Qing Yu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究超强耦合光-物质体系中量子精度的规范依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同规范（及其截断）给出不同物理预测，量子Fisher信息是否规范不变、边界在哪里尚不清楚。

Method: 分析规范间幺正变换含耦合 g、每个截断定义不同规范族；用量子Fisher信息跨规范作解析追踪，并比较谐波物质与最低耦合界。

Result: 量子Fisher信息在规范间呈精确抛物线；谐波物质在普适 θ*=Ω/(Ω+ω) 处最小；仅最低耦合界可达；不同规范相差9.6倍且趋势相反；Dicke点处虚激发增益发散是规范人为效应并饱和。

Conclusion: 量子精度并非规范无关，规范选择决定可达精度；Dicke点的发散不具物理意义。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the gauge dependence of quantum precision in ultrastrongly coupled light--matter systems. The unitary between gauges carries the coupling $g$, so each truncation defines a different family. The quantum Fisher information traces an exact parabola across frames. Harmonic matter minimizes it at a universal $θ_\ast=Ω/(Ω+ω)$, set by the matter and cavity frequencies. Only the minimal-coupling bound is attainable. Frames differ by a factor $9.6$ with opposite trends. Diverging virtual-excitation gain at the Dicke point is a gauge artifact and saturates.

</details>


### [112] [Universal two-qubit control across dilution refrigerators via a 10-km coherent optical link](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12481)
*Chuanlong Ma,Jiajun Chen,Jia-Qi Wang,Zheng-Xu Zhu,Jia-Hua Zou,Linze Li,Dingran Gu,Yuan-Hao Yang,Lintao Xiao,Xiaoxuan Pan,Xin-Biao Xu,Baile Chen,Haifeng Yu,Weiting Wang,Chang-Ling Zou,Luyan Sun*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出相干光链路（COL），在稀释制冷机间传输微波波形与相位参考，实现跨制冷机量子控制并纠缠两个量子比特，贝尔态保真度80.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式量子网络需要跨远距离制冷机的自适应经典链路来利用共享纠缠，但从独立制冷机远距离控制超导量子处理器仍难以实现。

Method: 构建COL：激光器+微波-光转换器+光-微波光电二极管，经10 km光纤传输微波控制信号；同一激光器产生光参考以补偿器件间频率失配。

Result: 通过COL向另一制冷机中的transmon比特与腔比特传递通用门集合，使二者纠缠，贝尔态保真度达80.6%。

Conclusion: COL提供的量子控制构成模块化量子处理器的经典骨干。

Abstract: Distributed quantum networks are important for scalable quantum processors and for potential applications in communication, distributed computation, and sensing, in which direct classical links for adaptive control are essential to harness the shared entanglement. However, controlling superconducting quantum processors from a separate refrigerator over long distances remains elusive. Here, we introduce a coherent optical link (COL) that delivers microwave waveforms and their phase references between dilution refrigerators. Combining a laser, a microwave-to-optical transducer and an optical-to-microwave photodiode, the COL transmits microwave control signals over 10~km of fiber, while optical references derived from the same laser compensate for the frequency mismatch between devices. Through the COL, we deliver a universal gate set to a transmon qubit and a cavity qubit in another refrigerator and entangle them, and achieve a Bell-state fidelity of 80.6\%. Quantum control delivered through the COL thus provides a classical backbone for modular quantum processors.

</details>


### [113] [An Alternative to Quantum Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12494)
*Julian Barbour,Maria I. R. Lourenço*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文提出一个整体性、尺度不变的替代本体论框架，仅以欧氏空间中点粒子的质量（电荷）比与相互间距比刻画"形状"，用一个尺度不变函数描述物理，不需动力学演化，试图在不引入标准量子力学的前提下重构量子理论的实验预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子理论虽在实验上极为成功，但测量诠释与纠缠关联的本质仍悬而未决；作者希望绕开这些概念困难，寻找一个更基础的描述。

Method: 假设宇宙仅由欧氏空间中的点粒子构成，粒子的唯一属性是质量（及电荷）之比和彼此间距之比（即只依赖"形状"）；理论由单个尺度不变函数表征，不含动力学演化，并可统一处理嵌入无限多粒子构型中的有限子系统。

Result: 摘要属纲领性论述，未给出定量结果；作者仅断言该框架在原理上能统一描述有限子系统，并可能导出原子、分子与宏观物体的观测结构。

Conclusion: 该尺度不变的整体性框架为重建量子实验结果提供了一条可能路径，并为原子、分子到宏观结构的涌现提供了新思路；但其可行性与定量预言尚待后续工作检验。

Abstract: Despite the remarkable empirical success of quantum theory, the interpretation of measurement and the nature of entanglement correlations remain open questions. We outline an alternative framework that seeks to recover the empirical results of quantum theory from a holistic, scale-invariant ontology. Assuming only point particles in Euclidean space, characterised by ratios of masses (and charges) and by the ratios of their mutual separations, the theory describes shapes through a single scale-invariant function; no dynamical evolution is required. In principle, this framework provides a unified description of finite subsystems embedded in a configuration of infinitely many particles and offers a possible route to deriving the observed structures of atoms, molecules and macroscopic bodies without invoking standard quantum mechanics.

</details>


### [114] [Singularising quantum channels preserving kappa-additivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12537)
*Sviatoslav V. Dzhenzher*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 通过关于可测阿贝尔群上κ-可加概率测度对酉平移共轭取平均，构造量子信道，并刻画其弱-*积分表示与输出态奇异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典"群上平均/卷积"思想迁移到算子代数框架，研究平移共轭平均所定义的量子信道在非完全可加（κ-可加）测度下的行为，并补足所需的积分理论工具。

Method: 在可测阿贝尔群上取κ-可加概率测度μ，对酉平移共轭映射T_x(·)=U_x(·)U_x^*作平均，构造作用于由平移算子与有界可测乘法算子生成的算子代数之对偶上的信道；考察其在κ-可加态类上的保持性与弱-*积分表示，并在附录证明κ-可加测度的Fubini定理。

Result: 信道保持量子态的κ-可加性，且在该类态上具有弱-*积分表示；若平均测度在单点集上取值为零（非原子），则每个输出态均为奇异态；附录给出κ-可加测度的相应Fubini定理。

Conclusion: 奠定了"平均型"（去相干/混合）量子信道在κ-可加测度与算子代数对偶设定下的系统框架，并指出非原子平均必然导致输出态奇异性这一结构性结论。

Abstract: We construct quantum channels by averaging unitary shift conjugations with respect to $κ$-additive probability measures on measurable abelian groups.
  The channels act on the duals of operator algebras generated by shifts and bounded measurable multiplication operators.
  They preserve $κ$-additivity of quantum states and admit a weak-$*$ integral representation on this class.
  If the averaging measure vanishes on singletons, every output state is singular.
  In the appendix, we also establish the corresponding Fubini's theorem for $κ$-additive measures.

</details>


### [115] [All-to-all connectivity via adiabatic ZZ interactions in a superconducting flux qubit processor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12576)
*Elena Lupo,Nguyen H. Le,Eran Ginossar,Michael Stern*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种基带驱动的绝热ZZ门，可在共腔耦合的通量量子比特寄存器中对任意选定比特对进行纠缠，无需谐振条件，数值模拟显示相干误差低于10^-4。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超导量子处理器通过可调耦合器连接近邻比特，需高连通性但带来大量布线与控制开销，因此需要一种能对共享腔中任意比特对直接实现纠缠、且控制开销低的方案。

Method: 比特保持直流偏置于通量甜点；通过局域磁通线施加频率远低于比特跃迁的强驱动，绝热倾斜量子化轴，产生有效纵向ZZ相互作用；耦合强度由驱动幅度与相对相位调节，而非依赖共振条件。

Result: 数值模拟给出在宽驱动频率范围内相干门误差低于10^-4；四比特寄存器中同时执行两个门时保真度相当；协议对频率涨落鲁棒，且在许多比特耦合到腔时仍有效。

Conclusion: 该基带绝热ZZ门为共腔通量比特架构提供了高连通性、低开销、可扩展的纠缠方案，具备高保真度与良好鲁棒性。

Abstract: Scalable superconducting quantum processors would benefit from high qubit connectivity, yet existing architectures connect neighbouring qubits via tunable couplers, which introduce substantial wiring and control overhead. Here, we propose a baseband-driven adiabatic ZZ gate that entangles any selected pair in a register of flux qubits coupled via a common cavity. The qubits stay dc-biased at their flux sweet spot. Strong drives applied selectively through local flux lines, at frequencies far below the qubit transitions, adiabatically tilt the quantization axes and generate an effective longitudinal ZZ interaction. The strength of the coupling is tunable and controlled by the amplitudes and relative phase of the drives rather than by any resonance condition. Numerical simulations give coherent gate errors below $10^{-4}$ over broad ranges of drive frequency, with comparable fidelities for two simultaneous gates in a four-qubit register. The protocol is robust against frequency fluctuations and remains effective even when many qubits are coupled to the cavity.

</details>


### [116] [Long-Range Ergotropy Transfer with Weakened Boundary-Coupling Spin Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12589)
*R. C. Krisnanda,M. S. Ukhtary,Abdurrouf*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究以边界弱耦合自旋链为媒介的能效（ergotropy）非局域传输，揭示边界弱化诱导的局域边缘态可提升传输保真度，并发现长链中传输能效随自旋数奇偶交替，最近邻与长程相互作用下弱化边界耦合均优于均匀链。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子电池的能效衡量最大可提取能量；当能量提取过程非局域时，需要将能效高保真地跨距离传输，而常规均匀自旋链难以兼顾长链情形下的高效传输，因此需寻找可行的链结构设计。

Method: 以两端耦合被弱化的自旋链作为传输媒介，分析其能谱结构以解释边缘局域态的形成；分别考虑最近邻相互作用与长程相互作用两类模型，比较弱化边界与均匀链的最大传输能效，并考察发送端初态对能效中相干/非相干成分输运及平均传输效率的影响。

Result: 弱化两端耦合产生局域边缘态，可在给定时间内实现高保真传输；长链下传输能效随自旋数奇偶交替，该奇偶效应可由链的能谱结构解释；在最近邻与长程相互作用下，弱化边界耦合对长链最大传输能效均优于均匀链；长程相互作用虽降低传输能效，但缩短传输时间，链越长越明显。

Conclusion: 边界弱耦合自旋链是提升长距离能效传输的有效方案，其性能源于边缘局域态与能谱奇偶结构；传输能效、传输时间与初态相关的相干性分配之间存在可调控的权衡，为量子电池的远程能量输运设计提供依据。

Abstract: Ergotropy is a key quantity of a quantum battery that measures the maximum extractable energy. When the extraction process is not local, a faithful transfer of ergotropy across distance is necessary. In this work, we investigate such transfer by using spin chains with weakened coupling at the boundaries as mediator. Weakening the coupling at the two boundaries of the chain gives rise to localized edge states allowing high fidelity transfer within given time duration. We found that, for long spin chains, the transferred ergotropy alternates between even and odd number of spins. We explain this parity effect by investigating the chain spectral structure. Furthermore, we consider both the nearest-neighbor and long-range interactions between spin. In both model of interactions, weakening the boundary couplings improves the maximum transferred ergotropy for long spin chain, compared with in the case of conventional uniform chain. Although the long-range interaction reduces the transferred ergotropy, the transfer time is reduced, too, in particular for longer chain. In addition to the total transferred ergotropy, we also investigate the effect of initial states of the sender spin to the transport of the coherent and incoherent components of the total ergotropy, and the average transport efficiency.

</details>


### [117] [Reducing Provers in Distributed Quantum Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12600)
*Alex Della Schiava,Atsuya Hasegawa,François Le Gall,Harumichi Nishimura*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究分布式量子 Merlin-Arthur（dQMA）模型中多纠缠证明者（k 个不可纠缠的证明者）情形下的证明大小与通信成本，并给出去纠缠（prover reduction）的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 Fraigniaud 等人提出的 dQMA 模型中，网络节点在不可信证明者的量子证明辅助下验证全局性质；节点计算能力无界，但需限制证明大小与验证通信。Hasegawa-Kundu-Nishimura（PODC 2024）要求证明在节点间不可纠缠，本文将其推广到 k 个不可纠缠证明者的 dQMAS(k) 模型，并研究每个节点的证明大小以及减少证明者数量所需的通信代价。

Method: 在 dQMAS(k) 模型下设计与分析证明者约减协议：(1) 将 k 个证明者减少到 2 个，本地证明大小加倍且不增加额外通信；(2) 利用额外 4 轮通信，将 k 个证明者减少到 1 个，本地证明大小至多为原来的 3 倍加上节点数的对数项；(3) 对完全完备（perfectly complete）协议建立放大（amplification）技术。

Result: k→2 的约减：证明大小增至 2 倍，通信量不变；k→1 的约减：额外 4 轮通信，本地证明大小 ≤ 3×原大小 + O(log n)（n 为节点数）；在完全完备协议情形下，通过放大可以用常数因子开销实现单证明者，从而得到 dQMAS = dQMAS(k)。

Conclusion: 多证明者的 dQMAS(k) 模型在证明规模与通信开销上可高效约减为常数个甚至单个证明者，且在完全完备协议下与单证明者模型等价（dQMAS = dQMAS(k)），说明增加不可纠缠证明者数量不会增强该类分布式量子验证模型的验证能力。

Abstract: In the distributed quantum Merlin-Arthur ($\mathsf{dQMA}$) model introduced by Fraigniaud, Le Gall, Nishimura, and Paz (ITCS 2021), a network of quantum computers verifies a global property with the aid of a quantum proof supplied by an untrusted prover. While the nodes are computationally unbounded, the challenge is to limit proof size and the communication required for verification. In this paper, we consider the model of Hasegawa, Kundu, and Nishimura (PODC 2024), where the proof must be unentangled across nodes, and define its extension to $k$ unentangled provers, denoted by $\mathsf{dQMAS}(k)$. In this setting, we investigate the proof size per node and the communication costs of reducing the number of provers.
  Our first result reduces the number of provers from $k$ to two, doubling the proof size without additional communication. Moreover, with four additional rounds, we can reduce the number of provers from $k$ to one, using at most three times the original local proof size plus a logarithmic term in the number of nodes. Finally, we establish amplification for perfectly complete protocols. In this regime, a single prover suffices with a constant-factor overhead in our reductions, yielding $\mathsf{dQMAS} = \mathsf{dQMAS}(k)$.

</details>


### [118] [Effective Time Advance of Quantum Clocks under Postselection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12609)
*Youka Kaku,Akira Matsumura,Tomohiro Fujita*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明量子后选择可通过弱值反常效应，使运动时钟相对静止时钟产生"有效时间提前"，逆转双生子佯谬中运动方更年轻的常规结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典双生子佯谬中运动一方因相对论时间膨胀而更年轻。作者希望探究量子层面的干涉与后选择能否改变这一结论，即是否存在有效时间提前的机制。

Method: 采用可解析求解的量子时钟模型：对质心运动进行后选择，通过反常弱值构造有效时间平移；随后推广到弱引力场，并考察简单相干态；最后以类氢原子为例，展示该效应在原子波函数中的表现形式。

Result: 尽管每条运动分支都经历普通的相对论时间膨胀，质心运动的后选择仍可产生有效时间提前；在弱引力场中，即使时钟运动处于简单相干态也能出现该效应，并在类氢原子波函数中可辨识。

Conclusion: 量子干涉与后选择可以产生超出常规相对论时间膨胀的有效时间提前，说明时间膨胀在量子后选择框架下并非不可逆，为量子时钟与引力/相对论交叉研究提供新视角。

Abstract: In the familiar twin paradox, the traveling twin ages less than the one who stays behind as a consequence of relativistic time dilation. Here, we show that quantum postselection can effectively reverse this outcome. Using an analytically solvable quantum clock model, we demonstrate that postselection of the center-of-mass motion produces an effective time advance through anomalous weak values, even though every individual motional branch undergoes ordinary time dilation. We extend this analysis to a weak gravitational field and show that an effective time advance can arise even for simple coherent states of the quantum clock motion. We further illustrate this effect using a hydrogen-like atom, revealing how it manifests itself in the atomic wavefunction. Our results demonstrate that quantum interference and postselection can produce an effective time advance beyond conventional relativistic time dilation.

</details>


### [119] [Quantum Residual Exploration for Selected Configuration Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12672)
*Kentaro Yamamoto,Ikko Hamamura,Juan W. Pedersen,Duncan Gowland,Gabriel Laude,Shintaro Maekawa,David Muñoz Ramo,Elica Kyoseva,William Dawson*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出模块化混合量子-经典方法，在固定最终行列式预算下改进选择性CI；量子测量提供的补充行列式按统一能量判据与经典候选取舍，并在Quantinuum Reimei离子阱装置上演示H12拉伸构型下相对纯经典选择降低变分能量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强关联体系中，基于微扰的行列式发现（如多步激发）容易遗漏，导致选择性CI在有限行列式预算下能量精度受限。

Method: 模块化量子-经典混合流程：经典部分进行选择性CI筛选；量子测量生成候选行列式；两类候选在共同能量判据下统一排序，仅当量子提出者排名优于经典候选时才被接纳，从而保证行列式预算不变。

Result: 在Quantinuum Reimei离子阱量子计算机上对拉伸几何H12测试，相同最终行列式预算下，含量子测量的方案变分能量低于纯经典选择。

Conclusion: 量子测量可作为对经典微扰发现的互补补充，在资源受限下提升选择性CI精度；结果支持混合方法在强关联问题上的可行性，但需进一步检验可扩展性与噪声影响。

Abstract: We propose a modular hybrid quantum-classical method to improve selected configuration interaction at a fixed final determinant budget. Quantum measurements supply complementary determinants, accepted only when they outrank classical candidates under a common energetic criterion. This targets multistep excitations that perturbative discovery may miss in strongly correlated systems. A demonstration on the Quantinuum Reimei trapped-ion quantum computer shows that measured proposals for H$_{12}$ at a stretched geometry lower the variational energy relative to classical-only selection at the same final determinant budget.

</details>


### [120] [No Exponential Quantum Advantage for Decoded Quantum Interferometry with Local Quantum Decoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12718)
*Brandon Augustino,Shouvanik Chakrabarti,Enrico Fontana,Dylan Herman,Guneykan Ozgul,Ruslan Shaydulin,Shreehari Sureshbabu,Jacob Watkins*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究解码量子干涉（DQI）在随机 D-正则 k-稀疏实例上的解码阈值，揭示了局部解码器受限于树重建阈值、最优解码器受限于凝聚阈值，并指出只有高效非局域解码器才是实现量子优势的可行路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DQI 将稀疏线性约束优化及其 Gibbs 采样归约为从量子态解码线性码，而实际常用的 BPQM 等局部解码器仅在子对数距离内估计码字符号，其性能极限与物理意义尚不清晰，需要明确 DQI 解码问题的阈值及其对量子优势的影响。

Method: 将 DQI 诱导的解码阈值对应到统计物理中的 Gibbs 测度相变：局部解码器对应树重建阈值，Max-k-XORSAT 最优解码器对应凝聚阈值。基于此证明信息论阻塞（无局部解码器可在树重建阈值之上高概率恢复码字），并证明基于 BPQM 的解码器可达该阈值；同时分析 pretty-good measurement 最优解码器恰在凝聚阈值之下成功。

Result: 证明了两项主要结果：(1) 树重建阈值以上不存在能高概率恢复码字的局部解码器，而 BPQM 解码器恰好达到该阈值；(2) 最优解码器（pretty-good measurement）恰在凝聚阈值之下成功，凝聚阈值为 DQI 性能提供紧上界，严格改进了 Jordan 等人由 Holevo 界导出的极限。

Conclusion: 结合腔方法关于局部 Markov 链在同样阈值内快速平衡的预测，DQI 配合局部解码器在采样上无指数加速、在依赖违反约束数的振幅函数下也无近似优化优势；实现量子优势的唯一可行路径是设计在两个阈值之间仍能成功的高效非局域解码器。

Abstract: Decoded quantum interferometry (DQI) reduces optimization of sparse linear constraint problems, and sampling from their Gibbs measures, to decoding a linear code from a quantum state. We study DQI on random $D$-regular $k$-sparse instances with $k\ge3$, for which local decoders such as belief propagation with quantum messages (BPQM) are commonly used. Such decoders estimate a typical codeword symbol using only the qudits within logarithmic distance of it. Our main insight is that the thresholds of the decoding problem induced by DQI correspond to phase transitions of the Gibbs measure that are well studied in statistical physics: tree reconstruction for local decoders and, for Max-$k$-XORSAT, condensation for the optimal decoder. This insight allows us to prove two main results. First, we prove an information-theoretic obstruction: no local decoder can recover the codeword with high probability above the tree reconstruction threshold. Conversely, we show that a decoder based on BPQM reaches this threshold. Since the cavity method predicts that local Markov chains equilibrate rapidly up to the same threshold, we expect DQI with local decoders to give no exponential speedup for sampling and no advantage in approximate optimization with amplitude functions that depend only on the number of violated constraints. Second, we consider the optimal decoder (the pretty-good measurement, for which no efficient implementation is known) and show that it succeeds exactly below the condensation threshold. The condensation threshold thus provides a tight upper bound on the performance of DQI with these amplitude functions, providing a strict refinement of the limit that Jordan et al.\ derived from the Holevo bound. Our results point to efficient nonlocal decoders that succeed between the two thresholds as the only viable path to a quantum advantage with DQI on these instances.

</details>


### [121] [Quantum Robustness via Sandwiched Rényi Divergences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12738)
*Luc Rey-Bellet*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文研究"近似已知量子态下可观测期望的最大偏差"问题：对任意阶 α∈[1/2,∞]，给出参考态在 sandwiched Rényi 散度预算内的最坏期望，并证明该量子问题可经典化为观测谱区间上的一个概率测度 ω_α，从而使经典概率的 Hoeffding、Bernstein、Markov–Krein 界直接适用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际中量子态只能近似获知，需要量化由此引起的可观测期望误差。已有工作多局限于特定散度或特定不等式，缺乏对全阶 α 统一的、可借力经典浓度不等式的框架。

Method: 以 sandwiched Rényi 散度为预算定义最坏期望；用凸对偶将其化为对 sandwiched Schatten 范数的一维优化（α=1/2 为 fidelity risk，α=1 为 entropic risk，α=∞ 为量子 CVaR）；核心是经典化定理：借助 Heinävaara 的迹联合谱测度构造观测谱区间上的测度 ω_α（α=1 时由 Stahl 定理给出），证明量子问题与经典问题一致。

Result: 量子问题等价于测度 ω_α 下的经典问题，故 Hoeffding、Bernstein、Markov–Krein 界及 Pinsker 型不等式（含 Bernstein 型改进）均可迁移至量子情形；仅在 α=1/2 时 ω_α 为 Born 测度，α>1/2 时可观测与态的非对易性使方差严格低于 Born 方差，导致所有方差敏感界被收紧（quantum deflation）。

Conclusion: 全阶统一的最坏期望刻画得以建立，经典浓度不等式可无损移植到量子设定；非对易性带来可量化的"涨落收缩"效应，为量子态估计中的误差界提供更紧的工具。

Abstract: If a quantum state is known only approximately, how much can the expectation of an observable change? We answer with the worst-case expectation over all states within a sandwiched Rényi divergence budget of a reference state, for every order $α\in [1/2,\infty]$. Convex duality gives this value as a one-dimensional optimization over a sandwiched Schatten norm, running from a fidelity risk at $α= 1/2$, through the entropic risk at $α= 1$, to a quantum conditional value-at-risk at $α= \infty$. The main result is a classicalization theorem: the quantum problem coincides with the classical one for a probability measure $ω_α$ on the spectral interval of the observable, constructed from Heinävaara's tracial joint spectral measure and given by Stahl's theorem at $α= 1$. The Hoeffding, Bernstein and Markov--Krein bounds of classical probability thereby apply to the quantum problem, and yield Pinsker-type inequalities with Bernstein-type refinements. The measure $ω_α$ is the Born measure only at $α= 1/2$; for $α> 1/2$, noncommutativity of the observable with the state strictly lowers its variance below the Born variance, and with it every variance-sensitive bound: a quantum deflation.

</details>


### [122] [An efficient classical algorithm for OTOCs in chaotic quantum dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12773)
*Yunchao Liu,Yihui Quek,Shreya Vardhan,Tianci Zhou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出并实现了一个多项式时间经典算法，可在典型一维随机量子电路中估计四点 OTOC^(1) 与八点 OTOC^(2)，表明在涨落剧烈的敏感区，OTOC 的平均情形估计是经典可处理的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在单个随机量子电路实例中估计 OTOC 被视为可验证量子优势的候选任务，且已被证明对张量网络、蒙特卡洛等传统经典算法困难。作者希望检验这种困难是否在平均情形下依然成立。

Method: 借助量子混沌的洞察，将 OTOC 组织为快速收敛的级数：领头项来自复制希尔伯特空间中置换自由度内的历史贡献，次领头项来自该空间之外的偏离。算法利用混沌引起的退相位效应抑制次领头项，并给出理论与数值证据。

Result: 理论与数值证据支持次领头项的抑制；实际实现可在高精度下估计一维电路的 OTOC^(1) 与 OTOC^(2)，且相比 Google 近期二维实验，在更深电路深度下获得更高信噪比。

Conclusion: 混沌并非经典模拟不可逾越的障碍，反而通过退相位使算法可行；将算法推广到更高维度、厘清二维 OTOC 的量子优势问题仍是开放问题。

Abstract: Estimating out-of-time-ordered correlators (OTOCs) in individual instances of random quantum circuits has been proposed as a candidate for verifiable quantum advantage, and demonstrated to be hard for conventional classical algorithms such as tensor networks and Monte Carlo. Using insights from quantum chaos, we develop and implement a polynomial-time classical algorithm for the four-point ${\rm OTOC}^{(1)}$ and the eight-point ${\rm OTOC}^{(2)}$ in typical instances of one-dimensional (1D) quantum circuits. Our results demonstrate average-case classical tractability of estimating OTOCs in the sensitive regime where circuit-to-circuit fluctuations are large. Our algorithm organizes the OTOC into a rapidly converging series, where the leading term includes contributions from histories within permutation degrees of freedom in the replicated Hilbert space, and subleading terms arise from excursions outside this space. Although chaos is often viewed as an insurmountable barrier to classical simulation, our algorithm exploits a form of dephasing caused by chaos, which suppresses the subleading terms. We provide strong theoretical and numerical evidence to support this suppression. Further, we demonstrate that practical implementations of our algorithm estimate ${\rm OTOC}^{(1)}$ and ${\rm OTOC}^{(2)}$ in 1D circuits with high accuracy, achieving higher signal-to-noise ratio at larger circuit depths compared to recent 2D experiments by Google. Extending our algorithm to higher dimensions, and resolving the status of quantum advantage in 2D OTOCs, remain open problems.

</details>
